## Document 395: Quantum dictionary, part 1_ Decoding the fundamentals From superposition to decoherence, we unpack the concepts and technologies shaping the future of quantum computing. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/Quantum dictionary, part 1_ Decoding the fundamentals From superposition to decoherence, we unpack the concepts and technologies shaping the future of quantum computing. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

13030420     1

Transformation 
Quantum dictionary, part 1: Decoding the fundamentals 
21 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• What is quantum computing? How does it work? What is a qubit? And when will quantum reach its full potential? Quantum 
computing challenges our intuition and our understanding of what computers can do. In this two-part dictionary series, we 
decode key terms behind what could be the next major technological revolution. 
• Qubits are the basic building blocks of quantum computers, harnessing properties such as superposition and entanglement to 
process information. However, qubits are highly sensitive to their surroundings where disturbances can lead to errors in 
quantum computation, making error correction a key challenge for building quantum computers. 
• There is no single approach to building a quantum computer. Researchers and companies are pursuing a range of qubit 
technologies, from neutral atoms and photons to spin, superconducting, topological and trapped-ion qubits, while quantum 
computing itself is likely to increasingly work alongside classical computing and AI.

First, let’s lay the groundwork… 
Quantum computing  
Quantum computing uses qubits and quantum properties such as superposition (defined later on) to perform complex 
calculations and solve certain problems faster than classical computers.1 From a practical perspective, quantum computing could 
be used to tackle problems related to optimization, machine learning, simulation and factorization across a range of sectors, 
including autos and logistics, financial services, energy and climate, health and life sciences and national security. 
Current quantum computers can perform ‘just’ thousands of quantum operations. As the technology advances from thousands to 
millions, billions and eventually trillions of operations, quantum computers could begin to have commercial applications.  
• Millions: Financial services and security could experience some of the first use cases, such as threat detection, fraud 
prevention, derivatives pricing, bond trading and arbitrage. 
• Billions: Quantum could help power AI through advanced data generation capabilities.  
• Trillions: Quantum chemistry applications could emerge, including drug discovery, battery simulation and materials 
discovery. 
These are the publicly known applications, according to Oxford Quantum Circuits (OQC). And as quantum computing capabilities 
improve, some of these use cases could emerge sooner than anticipated.  
Now, we’ll tackle the core concepts… 
Qubits 
Qubits (quantum bits) are a quantum computer’s basic unit of information. They can exhibit quantum properties such as 
superposition and entanglement (which we elaborate on below) — properties fundamental to quantum computing. 
Quantum gates manipulate the state of qubits, much like logic gates manipulate bits in classical computers. Despite the name, a 
quantum gate is not a physical object, but rather an operation applied to a quantum system that changes its state. 
From physical to logical qubits 
Physical qubits are the foundation of a quantum computer’s hardware. However, they are highly susceptible to error. To address 
this, multiple physical qubits can be structured together to encode a logical qubit, allowing errors to be detected and corrected

1 Schneider, J., & Smalley, I. (2025, June 10). What is quantum computing? IBM. 
I NSTI TUTE
Accessible version

### Page 2

2 21 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

and making the stored quantum information more reliable. In recent years, according to BofA Global Research, logical qubits 
have become a greater focus for quantum computing companies. 
Entanglement 
Entanglement is a condition where two or more qubits become linked so that their states are correlated, regardless of distance 
(Exhibit 1). It allows quantum computers to process information in ways that can be difficult or impossible for classical 
computers. 
So, how does it work? Suppose we have two photons that have opposite polarizations (the orientation of their light waves). 
Before measurement, neither has a definite polarization. When one is measured as horizontally polarized, the other will be 
vertically polarized when measured in the same basis, wherever it is. When measured separately, they appear to be random, but 
when compared, they are correlated (Exhibit 2). 
The challenges 
Once created, entanglement must be protected from decoherence (where interactions with the environment destroy quantum 
correlations). Maintaining entanglement over long distances is another challenge. Photons traveling through optical fiber can be 
absorbed or scattered, making long-distance quantum communication difficult. 2 Quantum repeaters aim to overcome this 
challenge by extending entanglement across a series of shorter links. 
Exhibit 1: Entanglement is a state where at least two qubits become 
correlated 
An infographic of entangled qubits

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Exhibit 2: Measuring one qubit in an entangled pair instantly 
determines the state of the other, no matter the distance 
An example of what entanglement means for qubit measurement

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Superposition 
Unlike a classical bit, which can be one of two states (0 or 1), superposition allows a quantum system to exist in multiple states 
or configurations at the same time (Exhibit 3). For example, a qubit can exist in the 0 state, the 1 state or any state that is a 
combination of 0 and 1.3  
A quantum system can maintain a superposition until it is observed or measured. Once measured, its superposition collapses into 
a single state. Another way of thinking about superposition is to imagine a sphere with the north pole representing zero and the 
south pole representing one. Other points on the surface of the sphere represent different superposition states. This allows us 
to: 1) represent complex forms of information; and 2) simulate atoms in a computer. 
Schrödinger’s cat 
In 1935, physicist Erwin Schrödinger proposed a thought experiment to illustrate the strange implications of quantum 
mechanics. In this scenario, a cat, a radioactive substance that decays very slowly, and a Geiger counter are put together in a 
sealed box. According to quantum mechanics, the system can be described as a superposition in which the cat is both alive and 
dead until the box is opened. Once the box is opened and the system is observed, the superposition collapses into a single 
outcome.

2 Rehman, M. (2026, May 11). What is Quantum Entanglement? Quantum Insider. 
3 Microsoft. (n.d.). Superposition.

### Page 3

21 October 2026    3

I NSTI TUTE

Enabling parallel computation versus classical computing 
Superposition is a key attribute of quantum computing that allows for quantum parallelism, or the ability to perform multiple 
computations at the same time. Classical bits can only be in one of two states, 0 or 1. In contrast, a qubit can be in a 
superposition of 0 and 1, allowing a quantum computer to process multiple possible qubit states at once.4  
Exhibit 3: Superposition is the condition where a qubit can exist in a superposition of 0 and 1 
Illustrating the power of superposition

Source: BofA Global Research 
Note: A Bloch sphere is a geometric representation of qubit states. 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Error rates and fidelity 
Fidelity is a measure of how closely a quantum state or operation matches what was intended (Exhibit 4). A higher fidelity 
generally means fewer errors. 
Physical qubits are prone to errors. One way to address this is to combine multiple physical qubits to create a more reliable 
logical qubit. Quantum error correction (QEC) allows errors in the physical qubits to be detected and corrected, helping protect 
the information stored in the logical qubit.

4 Ibid.

### Page 4

4 21 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

Exhibit 4: Fidelity measures how accurately a qubit retains its intended quantum state during computation 
Infographic showing the implications of high versus low fidelity

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
How does quantum error correction work? 
At its simplest, QEC relies on redundancy, or storing information across multiple qubits so that errors can be detected and 
corrected.  
Consider an example: suppose someone wants to send a message represented by 0. Instead of sending a single 0, the message 
could be repeated three times as 000, known as a codeword. If one bit were accidentally flipped from 0 to 1 during transmission, 
the recipient might receive 010. Because two of the three bits are still 0, the recipient could infer that the intended message 
was 000. 
However, if two bits flipped, producing 110 or 011, the recipient could infer the wrong message. If all three flipped to 111, the 
recipient would have no way to know from the received codeword that errors had occurred.5 This approach is known as a 
repetition code and illustrates how redundancy can help protect information from errors.  
Repetition codes are a simple form of error correction that can address bit-flip errors, where a 0 changes to a 1 or vice versa. 
More sophisticated QEC codes are needed to address other quantum errors, such as phase flips.6 Shor’s code, developed by 
American theoretical computer scientist Peter Shor, uses nine physical qubits to encode one logical qubit and can correct one 
bit-flip error or one phase flip error, but it can’t correct both types of errors at the same time. Meanwhile, Steane’s seven-qubit 
code, developed by Oxford physicist Andrew Steane, can also correct both types of errors. It has the advantage of being fault-
tolerant, meaning the error-correction process itself does not introduce additional errors.7 
At what error level might we start seeing quantum advantage? 
Some classical computing systems today operate with extremely high reliability, often described as having "17 nines" of fidelity 
(99.99999999999999999%). By comparison, quantum computers currently achieve fidelity rates of around 99.9%. Gerald 
Mullally, CEO of OQC, has argued that quantum systems would need to add roughly three more nines of fidelity to achieve 
commercial relevance, or quantum advantage. 
Noise and decoherence 
Noise refers to unwanted disturbances that affect quantum systems and can lead to errors in computations (Exhibit 5). It can 
come from sources such as interactions with the environment, electromagnetic interference and temperature fluctuations. 
Coherence is a quantum system’s ability to maintain its quantum properties, including superposition and entanglement. When 
coherence is lost through interactions with the environment, the process is known as “decoherence,” causing the system to 
behave more classically (Exhibit 6).8

5 Microsoft Ignite. (n.d.) Introduction to quantum error correction. 
6 Ibid. 
7 Ibid. 
8 Quandela. (n.d.) Quantum Decoherence.

### Page 5

21 October 2026    5

I NSTI TUTE

To fully control qubits for calculations and information transfer, they need to be carefully protected from their surroundings. 
External factors like noise, energy, changes in Earth’s magnetic field, radiation from Wi-Fi or mobile phones or cosmic rays can 
interfere with the process. Even small disturbances can lead to decoherence, making it difficult to develop large-scale, fault-
tolerant quantum computers.9 
According to QuEra Computing, quantum noise can be mitigated through physical isolation of qubits, the development of more 
precise control techniques and quantum error correction, which helps detect and correct errors in physical qubits.  
Exhibit 5: Noise refers to unwanted disturbances, e.g., interactions 
with the environment, that lead to errors in quantum computation 
An infographic explaining quantum noise

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Exhibit 6: Decoherence is when qubits lose their key quantum 
properties, superposition and entanglement 
An infographic of decoherence

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Quantum processing unit (QPU) 
Referred to as the brain of the quantum computer, the QPU is the core hardware where qubits reside and where quantum 
computations take place. QPUs rely on quantum behaviors like superposition and entanglement to tackle problems in areas such 
as cryptography, simulation and optimization. Just as a CPU (central processing unit) in classical computing contains multiple 
components, a QPU includes quantum gates (operations that manipulate qubits in a controlled way, similar to logic gates in 
classical computing) and interconnects, which enable communication between qubits and other quantum processors.10 
Next, we’ll look at broader computing architecture… 
Heterogeneous computing  
Heterogeneous computing refers to a future computing landscape likely combining classical and quantum computing, potentially 
unlocking more powerful, faster and cheaper compute and helping solve problems that classical computers cannot. According to 
BofA Global Research, AI and quantum computing could also converge and complement each other, enabling more 
transformative use cases. 
Quantum modality  
A quantum modality refers to the type of physical technology used to create and manipulate qubits. We discuss the modalities in 
greater depth in the following section. 
Lastly, we outline qubit technologies… 
Neutral atom qubit 
A quantum modality that uses individual atoms with no electrical charge as qubits (Exhibit 7). The atoms are held in place using 
focused lasers known as optical tweezers. Neutral atom qubit systems do not require atoms to be ionized, which means fewer 
energy constraints and potentially simpler cooling requirements. Atoms such as rubidium or cesium are typically used. The qubits 
are manipulated by changing the energy levels or positions of the trapped atoms using laser pulses.

9 QuEra. (n.d.) Quantum Noise. 
10 SpinQ. (2025, January 16). What does a Quantum Computer Look Like? Explain Key Parts.

### Page 6

6 21 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

Exhibit 7: A quantum modality where atoms with a net electrical charge of zero are the foundation for qubits 
Key features of neutral atoms

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Photonic qubit 
A quantum modality that uses particles of light, known as photons, to carry quantum information (Exhibit 8).11 Optical 
components such as beam splitters, which divide light along different paths, and phase shifters, which change the phase of light, 
can be used to perform quantum operations. Photonic quantum computers can use integrated photonic chips to manipulate 
photons and enable quantum logic. 
Exhibit 8: A quantum modality relying on the quantum properties of light 
Key features of photonic qubits

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

11 Moore, J. (2025, March 28). The 6 different types of quantum computing technology. TechTarget.

### Page 7

21 October 2026    7

I NSTI TUTE

Spin qubit 
A quantum modality that uses the intrinsic spin of electrons or nuclei in solid-state materials, such as silicon or diamond. These 
spins can be manipulated using magnetic or electric fields, and their states are read using optical or charge-sensing techniques. 
Spin qubits are particularly appealing because they can leverage existing CMOS (complementary metal-oxide semiconductor; a 
technology to build processors) manufacturing infrastructure, making it easier to integrate them with conventional processors. 
Superconducting qubit 
A quantum modality in which electronic circuits create qubits. They are built using Josephson junctions, where two 
superconductors are separated by a thin insulating layer. Microwave pulses manipulate the qubits’ quantum states to run 
computations.12  
Specifically, a resistance-free current oscillates back and forth around a quantum loop. An injected microwave signal excites the 
current into superposition states.13 To enable this process, superconducting materials are cooled to near absolute zero (around 
10-20 millikelvin) using dilution refrigerators, helping reduce thermal noise and maintain coherence. 
Topological qubit 
A quantum modality that aims to encode quantum information using topological properties rather than relying only on individual 
particle states (Exhibit 9). One way to think about this is through the paths certain quasiparticles take as they move around one 
another, creating “braids” in space-time. Because the information can depend on the overall pattern of these braids rather than 
small details of their paths, the approach is designed to be more resilient to certain disturbances. 
Exhibit 9: Resilient to small perturbations but requires special quasiparticles not yet been conclusively demonstrated in nature 
An infographic of topological qubits

Source: BofA Global Research

Trapped-ion qubit  
A quantum modality in which ions (electrically charged atoms) are held in place using electromagnetic fields. These ions act as 
the qubits and are controlled by the system with tightly focused laser beams.14 The qubits are held inside ultra-high vacuum 
(UHV) chambers.

Global backdrop

The global landscape remains complex, 
shaped by the interplay of AI, energy 
prices and tariffs, with significant 
implications for growth, inflation and 
financial conditions.

1) AI

The debate over the durability of the AI 
investment boom continues.  Tailwinds 
include technological advances, broader 
adoption, and s trategic and geopolitical 
considerations. Headwinds range from 
uncertainty over investment returns  and 
hyperscaler financing pressure to 
physical bottlenecks , safety concerns, 
and regulatory uncertainty.

Hyperscaler financing pressure has 
attracted increasing attention . In the 
early stages of the AI investment boom, 
hyperscaler capex was largely financed 
by internal cash flow. This is now 
changing, as free cash flow is expected 
to turn negative from 4Q, requiring major 
players to rely on debt issuance and 
other external financing increasingly.

At the same time, higher global interest 
rates are raising the cost of AI 
investment. The Fed resumed rate hikes 
at its September meeting, while global 
bond yields remain under upward 
pressure amid persistent inflation and 
elevated public and private borro wing 
needs. Our baseline assumes US rates 
will rise by 50bps this year and a further 
25bps in 2027 to 4.50%, with global 
long-term rates also remaining under 
pressure. This could increasingly weigh 
on the economics and pace of AI 
investment.

US-China AI competition is also drawing 
increasing attention.  Across the five 
layers of the AI ecosystem, China is 
making rapid progress in energy, 
infrastructure, AI models and 
applications, supported by its large scale, 
cost-effective domestic manufacturing 
supply chains and huge consumer 
market, which provides a wide range of 
real-world scenarios for AI adoption. The 
emergence of a more integrated and 
cost-competitive Chinese AI ecosystem 
could increasingly challe nge the US 
ecosystem, which currently operates at a 
relatively higher cost.

China, however, remains relatively weak 
in semiconductors.  Around half of 
China's domestic AI chip market still 
relies on foreign suppliers, including 
lower-end NVIDIA chips. This 
semiconductor bottleneck remains a key 
constraint on China's AI development. In 
our view, Taiwanese semiconductor 
companies' competiti ve moat therefore 
remains largely intact in the near term.

through 3Q, while WSTS expects growth 
to moderate from 90% this year to 27 % 
in 2027.

2) Energy

The US -Iran conflict remains at a 
standoff, keeping the energy -price 
outlook highly uncertain.  New 
developments are likely after the US 
midterm elections in November, with 
both further escalation and de -
escalation possible. Crude oil prices have 
remained elevated at around 
USD100/bbl amid a persistent 
geopolitical risk premium . In contrast, 
refined product prices have risen more 
sharply as refining disruptions and 
tighter product logistics have amplified 
the supply shock. Natural gas prices 
have also increased sharply, reflecting 
disruptions to LNG supply and limited 
alternative shipping routes.

Taiwan has been actively diversifying 
its energy import sources to cushion the 
impact. LNG imports from Qatar have 
fallen to zero since April, while imports 
from the US and other suppliers have 
surged to offset the shortfall, helping 
maintain stable domestic electricity 
supply.

3) Tariffs

US tariff actions are likely to accelerate 
after the US midterm elections in 
November. Two major tariff measures 
remain in the pipeline. The Section 301 
forced-labor tariff was implemented in 
July, while final tariff action under the 
Section 301 overcapacity investigation, 
which covers 16 economies, remains 
pending. Meanwhile, Phase 1 of the 
Section 232 semiconductor tariffs was 
implemented in January, while Phase 2, 
covering a broader range of 
semiconductor-related products, 
remains pending.

Taiwan has secured a relatively 
favourable framework through its trade 
and investment agreements with the 
US. Taiwan signed an Agreement on 
Reciprocal Trade (ART) and an 
Investment MOU with the US earlier this 
year. The ART establishes a 15% US tariff 
rate on Taiwanese goods, while the MOU 
provides Taiwan with preferential 
treatment under Section 232, including 
for semiconductors. The ART has yet to 
enter into force, and it remains to be 
seen how the final Section 301 tariff 
burden will interact with the 15% ART 
framework. Meanwhile, major Taiwanese 
companies such as TSMC are expanding 
their US investments under the 
Investment MOU, which could support 
relatively favourable treatment for 
Taiwan when broader Section 232 
semiconductor tariffs are introduced.

Taiwan 4Q outlook

Taiwan has navigated the complex 
global environment relatively well so far 
this year, supported by robust AI exports 
and investment, diversified energy 
imports, and efforts to absorb higher 
upstream energy costs. Some 
normalisation is likely in 4Q26 –2027, as 
the AI -driven growth boost moderates 
and domestic energy prices face greater 
pressure to adjust.

1) Growth

GDP growth remains broadly on track 
with our expectations. High -frequency 
indicators suggest that GDP growth 
should remain around 12% yoy in 3Q, 
broadly in line with 12.9% in 2Q but below 
the 15.4% peak in 1Q. Growth is expected 
to moderate to a high -single-digit pace 
from 4Q onwards. We maintain our full -
year GDP growth forecasts at 11.6% for 
2026 and 5.6% for 2027.

The AI cycle remains the key driver of 
Taiwan's growth.  Information and 
communication products and electronic 
components accounted for nearly 80% 
of total exports in the first nine months 
of this year, while manufacturing 
accounts for nearly 50% of gross fixed

capital formation. From 4Q onwards, 
export growth is expected to moderate 
from exceptionally strong levels to a 
more normal but still robust pace, 
broadly following the trajectory of 
global hyperscaler capex and 
semiconductor sales. Investment is likely 
to follow a similar path.

For now, Taiwanese technology 
companies continue to report robust 
revenue growth, led by semiconductors, 
ODMs/EMS, and PCs and servers. 
Capacity utilisation at leading Taiwanese 
foundries has also continued to rise, 
suggesting that investment demand 
remains strong.

The AI boom is also beginning to 
generate broader spillovers to the 
domestic economy. The AI-driven equity 
rally has generated significant wealth 
effects, with the TAIEX up 70% year-to-
date and Taiwan becoming the world's 
fifth-largest stock market. Taiwanese 
households have benefited from the 
appreciation of financial assets, with 
equities and mutual funds accounting for 
nearly 30% of total household financial 
assets. The equity rally has also 
generated positive income effects. Wage 
growth has picked up, led by the services 
sector, particularly financial services, as 
strong earnings at financial 
institutions—especially securities 
firms—have prompted higher 
compensation to retain talent. These 
wealth and income gains should support 
household consumption, creating a 
positive feedback loop that broadens the 
benefits of the AI boom across the wider 
economy.

2) Inflation

CPI inflation is also tracking broadly in 
line with our forecast. Headline CPI 
remained elevated at 2.4% yoy in 3Q, up 
from 1.2% in 1Q and 2.2% in 2Q. We 
maintain our forecast for headline CPI 
inflation to remain close to 2.5% in 4Q, 
and retain our full-year forecasts at 2.0% 
for 2026 and 2.1% for 2027.

Energy inflation remains the main 
upside risk.  The government has 
absorbed much of the increase in energy 
costs through state -owned CPC and 
Taipower, limiting the pass -through to 
domestic consumers. With losses at both 
companies widening, the government 
will need to decide whether to provide 
additional subsidies to cover the losses 
or allow further increases in domestic 
energy prices. The September re view 
postponed the adjustment to the 
December meeting, when the overall 
situation will be reassessed after the 
November local elections. Taipower's  
latest review indicated that electricity 
rates would need to rise by 12.8%. A hike 
in electricity prices could lift inflation 
expectations and generate second -
round effects, particularly as the labour 
market tightens and wage pressures 
increase.

3) Monetary policy

The central bank kept the policy 
discount rate unchanged at 2.00% at its 
September meeting, in line with our 
expectations. We continue to expect a 
modest 12.5bp rate hike at the 
December meeting, bringing the policy 
rate to 2.125%.  By then, CPI inflation is 
likely to have remained above 2% for 
around six consecutive months. The 
possibility of an increase in electricity 
prices following the November local 
elections could further lift inflation 
expectations, strengthening the case for 
a December rate hike.

Financial conditions have already 
tightened despite the absence of a 
policy rate hike.  M2 growth remained 
around 7% yoy in 3Q, well below double-
digit nominal GDP growth. At the same 
time, bank loan growth has accelerated 
to more than 10% yoy, driven by strong

                                                                                                                                            8

corporate credit demand, particularly AI-
related fundraising by technology 
companies. Tighter financial conditions 
are also evident in rising market interest 
rates, including commercial paper rates 
and government bond yields.

The central bank has continued to ease 
housing credit controls gradually. At its 
September meeting, it raised the LTV 
cap on second -home mortgage loans to 
70%, the second easing of housing credit 
controls this year. There is scope for 
further easing, including removing the 
LTV cap on second-home loans or raising 
the LTV caps for third - and fourth-home 
loans and high -value properties, which 
currently stand at 30%. However, the 
central bank is likely to proceed 
cautiously, given concerns that a faster 
relaxation could reignite  speculative 
demand and put renewed upward 
pressure on housing prices.

4) Housing

A gradual and modest recovery in the 
housing market is expected from 4Q26 
into 2027.  The existing -home market 
has already shown early signs of 
recovery, with Sinyi  housing prices 
returning to slightly positive yoy growth 
since 2Q. Total building transactions also 
returned to positive yoy growth in 3Q. 
The easing of credit controls should help 
reinforce this modest recovery trend.

The new -home market remains under 
pressure. The number of new units under 
construction has started to decline since 
2023, while new completions are  only 
beginning to ease this year . Demand 
remains weak, as potential buyers 
continue to wait for lower prices. Unsold 
new homes stood at 113K units at end -
2025, compared with a historical normal 
level of around 80K, implying excess 
inventory of roughly 30K units. At the 
current pace of new -home transactions, 
it would take roughly one year to 
normalize the existing excess inventory, 
and longer when factoring in the new 
supply pipeline. Some industry experts 
estimate that it could take as long as 3.8 
years digest current inventories fully 
(here).

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
WESTPAC NOWCAST Q3
2026: SECOND ESTIMATE
Growth falters through the quarter
16 October 2026
Key points
• The second estimate from Westpac-Now
points to GDP growth of around +0.5%qtr
in Q3 2026 and 2.1%yr. This is slightly lower
than the first estimate of +0.6%qtr. This

still represents a slight step up in growth
from Q2 2026 but masks some loss of
momentum over the course of the quarter.
• Our Monthly Activity Index deteriorated
noticeably in August and September.
Weakness has become increasingly
broad-based, with labour and business
condition indicators among the
largest drags and relatively few areas
providing a meaningful offset.
• The economy is coming under increasing
pressure as the cumulative impact of
higher interest rates combines with

renewed disruptions in global energy
markets and a deepening housing
downturn. With another rate hike likely in
November, these pressures are expected
to build further heading into 2027.
• Overall, the economy is becoming
increasingly reliant on the upswing in
business investment to support growth.
This narrow base of growth has left the
balance of risks less favourable. Our central
case remains one of slowing rather than
stalling. Westpac-Now’s current model

dynamics point to growth moderating
to around +0.4%qtr in Q4 2026. Our
formal forecasts have growth slowing
to a softer +0.2%qtr pace (see here).
• Our final Westpac-Now estimate for Q3
2026 will be published on 20 November.

2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
In November 2025, we introduced Westpac-Now (see here),
a continuously-updated measure of current activity in the
Australian economy. Westpac-Now draws on more than
60 high frequency economic and financial variables, using
advanced statistical techniques to provide a timely pulse

check on economic momentum. It is the first Australian
model to incorporate internal banking data, enhancing its
predictive power.
This note provides the second estimate of GDP growth for the
September quarter 2026, using domestic and international
data released up to and including the September Labour Force
Survey (released 15 October 2026). Updates are published
monthly (see Appendix A for the release schedule).
Westpac Monthly Activity Index: activity falters as

headwinds mount
After showing tentative signs of improvement through the
second half of 2025, the Westpac Monthly Activity Index has
weakened markedly over 2026. The Index moved back into
negative territory in February and has declined further since.
While there were some signs of stabilisation around mid-year,
this proved to be short lived with the Index falling to –0.88 in
September, outside of the pandemic period, the weakest read
since 2013.
The economy is coming under pressure from multiple

fronts, with key headwinds coming from higher interest
rates, renewed disruptions to global energy markets and a
deepening in the housing downturn. Among households,
spending held up surprisingly well through the first half of the
year despite historically weak sentiment, but more recently
Growth momentum on shaky ground
spending outside of fuel has started to cool (see here). At
the same time, the labour market has continued to soften,
with unemployment reaching a cycle high of 4.7% (see here).

Meanwhile, the business sector is also showing signs of
softening, with sentiment deteriorating and conditions holding
at weak levels (see here). Business credit growth remains
a notable exception, although even here there are tentative
signs of easing (see here).
This deterioration is particularly evident in our heatmap.
Negative contributions have become both broader and more
pronounced through 2026, with the weakness intensifying
in August and September. Labour and business condition

indicators are now the largest sources of weakness, while
spending and confidence have also deteriorated materially.
Credit and capacity utilisation indicators have been
comparatively less negative, but there are currently few areas
providing a meaningful offset to the broader softening.
Our indicator heatmap decomposes changes in the Monthly
Activity Index into group-level contributions, allowing us to
assess which components are driving movements in activity.

For ease of interpretation, indicators are grouped into broad
categories (see the Appendix for further details). For example,
the labour market category includes both official ABS Labour
Force Survey outcomes and timelier survey-based measures of
labour demand and job advertisements.
Westpac-Now: Resilient growth outlook slowing not
stalling
Recall that Westpac-Now is a refined indicator that combines
our Monthly Activity Index with advanced econometric

techniques to estimate GDP growth. As previously noted,
the fit is not expected to be exact. Measured GDP includes
Neha Sharma
Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
components that are not well captured by high-frequency
activity indicators, such as imputed rents for owner-occupiers,
housing-related ownership transfer costs, public and business
inventories, and depreciation.
To bridge monthly and quarterly data, we use a MIDAS

framework, allowing us to combine mixed-frequency inputs
and incorporate partial information for the current quarter
when producing GDP forecasts.
Based on current model estimates, Westpac-Now’s second
estimate for Q3 2026 points to GDP growth of 0.5%qtr
(with a 70% confidence interval of –0.1%qtr to 1.0%qtr)
and 2.1%yr, representing a slight downgrade from the first
estimate of +0.6%qtr but still a step up from the 0.4%qtr
(2.1%yr) outcome recorded in Q2 2026. While aggregate

growth appears to have held up through Q3, the underlying
picture became softer as the quarter progressed. At the
second decimal place the estimate is 0.46%qtr, leaving it
close to rounding down to 0.4%qtr.
With several headwinds now bearing on the economy
simultaneously, there is a growing risk that the slowdown in
activity becomes more pronounced than currently anticipated.
At the same time, the strength of the business investment
upswing provides an important counterweight that should

help cushion the broader slowdown.
Looking ahead, model dynamics suggest growth will tick
down to 0.4%qtr in Q4 2026 before picking up in Q1 2027.
However, these projections remain much more tentative,
given their reliance on projected inputs rather than realised
data. In addition, heightened uncertainty, particularly around
the renewed tensions in the Middle East conflict, means risks
around these forward estimates are larger than usual.
September Quarter data: a softer end to the quarter

Data released in the first month of the September quarter
indicated that the economy has picked up momentum relative
to the previous quarter, with our first nowcast estimating Q3
growth at 0.6%qtr, in line with the 0.6%qtr “pure forecast”
based solely on data available up to June 2026.
However, the flow of data since then shows downside risks to
growth have begun to materialise. Incorporating the August,
followed by the September (to date), data both yielded

nowcasts of 0.5%qtr (with a slight softening at the second
decimal place). This contrasts with Q2 2026, when weakness
emerged early in the quarter, in April, before subsequent data
pointed to stabilisation.
Note that monthly activity estimates are more uncertain
than normal when inflation is shifting. Faster price growth
can cause nominal indicators, such as consumer spending,
to rise more strongly even though ‘real’, inflation-adjusted
spending may be softening (and vice versa). The inclusion

of ‘real’ indicators, such as employment and banking
transaction volumes, helps mitigate some – but not all – of this
uncertainty.
Bottom line:
Westpac-Now points to GDP growth of around 0.5%qtr
(2.1%yr) in Q3 2026, suggesting aggregate growth held up
reasonably well through the quarter, although the flow of
data points to softer momentum as the quarter progressed.
The substantial upswing in business investment remains
an important source of support, helping to offset mounting

weakness elsewhere in the economy.
Looking ahead, the accumulation of headwinds is expected to
see growth slow further towards year-end, with Westpac-Now
currently pointing to growth of around 0.4%qtr in Q4 2026.
Downside risks around the outlook have increased, but for now
our central case remains one of slower growth rather than a
material downturn.

Appendix A: Westpac-Now Release Schedule (2026)
Going forward, Westpac Now will be published on the Friday following each ABS Labour Force Survey (LFS) release, at 11:30am AEDT/AEST (Canberra time).
Each release incorporates the latest labour market data alongside other high frequency indicators and provides an updated nowcast for quarterly GDP growth.

As the quarter progresses, each update should be interpreted as a progressively more complete estimate of that quarter’s economic performance.
Release date (Friday) LFS reference month Quarter focus Interpretation
20 November 2026 October 2026 Q3 2026 – final estimate Near complete read on Q3 activity
18 December 2026 November 2026 Q4 2026 – first estimate Initial assessment of Q4 momentum

## Document 455: Why AT1 still matters in 2027

Source PDF: all report pdf/Why AT1 still matters in 2027.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

AT1 reform may remain on the back burner in 2027, leaving
the instruments' role in bank capital largely unchanged. Solid
fundamentals remain supportive, while supply should be
manageable – but tight(ish) spreads and low reset levels could
weigh on the market
Jesse Norcross
Bank capital enters 2027 on solid ground, while tight spreads and rising risks
favour selectivity
AT1 reform remains on the back burner
The European Commission published its report on competitiveness

in the banking sector in July but did not include much in the way of
proposals for AT1 bank capital instruments. The report mainly
focused on reducing complexity, reporting requirements and
improving competitiveness for Europe’s banking sector.
For AT1, this probably means that the instruments are unlikely to
be phased out anytime soon. What is somewhat relevant for AT1s
is the Commission’s intention to reduce complexity for bank capital
structures. This includes making the Pillar 2 requirement and

### Page 2

guidance more transparent, while the Commission also proposes to
consolidate parts of the buﬀers into two buﬀers: one that can be
released in periods of stress, and another that would be maintained
in all scenarios.
This, in theory, could reduce the risk of coupon skips, as being able
to release buﬀers during periods of stress would not automatically
lead to a breach of the combined buﬀer requirements. The
proposals also include a removal of the leverage ratio Pillar 2 capital

requirement, which would aﬀect the largest European systemically
important banks subject to this requirement. This is only a small
number of banks but could (in theory) lead to a marginal decline in
AT1 issuance.
Overall, AT1 reform remains part of a broader discussion, with the
EU Commission proposals also likely to change as negotiations
remain ongoing and consensus needs to be built. For AT1, we
expect the instruments to keep playing an important role (yay!) in

European banks’ capital stacks, with no signs of an imminent
abolition or grandfathering process.
More supply while reﬁnancing remains the
main event
With no major changes to AT1 structures or requirements
imminent, we don’t expect a signiﬁcant impact on banks’ capital
instrument management and funding plans in 2027.
Primary issuance in euro-denominated AT1 and Tier 2 has been in
line with expectations in 2026. EUR AT1 issuance has just passed
€15bn at the time of writing, slightly above our expectation of

€15bn. Similarly, EUR Tier 2 has reached €27bn, nearing our
expectation of €30bn for 2026.
In 2027, we expect a modest increase in supply, largely dominated
by the reﬁnancing of upcoming redemptions/calls. We expect
€20bn in AT1 supply, compared to €14bn of calls. While this
represents a step-up, incremental net supply is only around €3bn
higher than in 2026. Tier 2 redemptions will also rise by around

French banks: Déjà vu?
French sovereign risk has increased, with the 10-year OAT-Bund

spread now trading at around 160bp at the time of writing, the
highest level since 2011. The French 10y yield is around 5%, the
highest level since 2002. The story in French markets is growing
more sinister by the day. But contagion risk across the eurozone is
(so far) relatively contained. Italy and Belgium stand out as other
higher-risk countries, given high debt levels and deﬁcits, but it
remains a French story for now. Nonetheless, the domestic

sovereign exposure and the sovereign-bank nexus have come back
into play.
So far, the market is not questioning the broader future of the
eurozone. Still, France is of a much larger magnitude than countries
Cookie settings

### Page 4

such as Greece or even Italy, which had to be supported during the
eurozone crisis.
What can be said is that French banks, as well as the broader
European banking sector, are in much better shape than they were
during the eurozone crisis. French banks’ capital ratios have
increased signiﬁcantly and now stand at around 13-15% on
average, compared to c.9-10% during the eurozone crisis. Liquidity
levels are healthy, while earnings and exposures, particularly for

the larger banks, are well diversiﬁed. For instance, BNP Paribas
generated around 25% of its revenues in 2025 in France, while
French deposits account for 29% of the total. For Société Générale,
France accounts for 40% of exposure.
French banks’ direct exposure to OATs is also not that large; it
represents around 6% of total assets, while roughly two-thirds is
held at amortised cost, making holdings less sensitive to valuation
declines. Some French banks do carry higher exposure, particularly

those with limited geographic diversiﬁcation. For instance, La
Banque Postale shows the highest OAT exposure as a percentage of
CET1, at 114%. But around 95% is held at amortised cost.
On average, for most French banks, OAT holdings represent around
15-30% of CET1. For context, the data suggests that a 100bp
widening of the large French banks’ domestic sovereign holdings
would lower CET1 for BNP and Société Générale by less than 10bp,
and up to 25bp for Crédit Agricole.

manageable

Still, the risks are rising and sustained high OAT-Bund levels could
feed into the real economy, credit demand, conﬁdence and asset
quality. While non-performing loans (NPLs) are low for French
banks, they have risen a bit in recent quarters, and we could see

more procyclical provisioning from French banks, in particular for
SMEs and consumer loans.
Risks could also arise from other avenues. Moody’s surprisingly
maintained France’s rating at Aa3, with a negative outlook, despite
broad expectations of a downgrade. Nonetheless, rating
downgrades remain a risk, and S&P is due for review at the end of
November, bringing further sovereign ratings pressure.
A larger move in OATs would also be more disruptive, as higher

yields, social unrest and political fragmentation complicate deﬁcit
reduction. October’s preliminary composite PMI of 48.4 adds to the
weaker-growth picture.
However, we do not think this is the same sovereign-bank loop as in
2009-2012. European Central Bank funding facilities, the Bank
Recovery and Resolution Directive (BRRD) and stronger liquidity
coverage ratio (LCR) and net stable funding ratio (NSFR)
requirements have reduced banks’ dependence on sovereign

guarantees. The link remains: ratings, domestic bond holdings and
a shared operating environment still transmit sovereign stress into
bank funding costs and market access.

### Page 6

The ﬂip side is that liquidity requirements and favourable EU
sovereign risk weights have made banks important buyers of
government debt. As ECB purchases ended and TLTROs were
repaid, sovereign holdings have partly replaced central bank
reserves for LCR purposes. Banks rely less on sovereign guarantees,
but on the other hand, sovereigns arguably rely more on banks for
funding.
This new relationship, alongside expectations that the ECB would
contain broader spillovers, perhaps helps explain why bank spreads

have not widened as much as OATs. The ECB’s reﬁnancing and
bond purchase tools remain part of the toolkit, although they have
previously been used in a diﬀerent inﬂation and rates environment.
Without sovereign relief, pressure is likely to persist, particularly for
domestically focused banks with larger relative OAT holdings.
Limited issuance may slow the repricing, but the distinction
between issuers remains important, especially in AT1.
French AT1 underperforms

French AT1 has underperformed YTD and now trades wider than
most other major country cohorts in EUR AT1. That mostly reﬂects
heightened sovereign-bank nexus concerns and OAT-Bund spread
widening, rather than a deterioration in fundamentals. However, it
also reﬂects market concerns about potential ratings impacts and
ongoing political and ﬁscal volatility, as well as a weakening in the
macro environment.
We would avoid treating all French banks similarly. Sensitivity to

OATs and the French economy diﬀer quite a bit across issuers. As
such, the recent widening has been quite uniform, but the market
could become more selective in French AT1s if nervousness
continues into 2027. We think the most diversiﬁed names,
including BNP and ACAFP in short-call bonds, with high reset levels,
should hold up better.

levels in EUR

Calling remains the base case, while the
economics are changing
Banks have continued to call their AT1s this year, and calls (or
tenders) remain our base case for 2027. While the decision to call
or extend is highly dependent on market circumstances and, to a
lesser extent, driven by regulatory approval, we still see a high bar

for not calling/replacing.
AT1 reset levels have hit lows in 2026
Source: IHS Markit, ING

overall coupon levels are in line with recent historical averages. The
economics become less comfortable further out. Reset spreads
decline for bonds callable from 2030 onwards, leaving these bonds

AT1 and Tier 2: Spread widening at different
speeds
The EUR AT1 index has widened by 19bp year-to-date,
outperforming callable and bullet Tier 2. Over the last three
months, the picture looks diﬀerent, though. AT1 has
underperformed Tier 2 as recent market stress has led to quite a bit

of widening. On a beta-adjusted basis, AT1 has had a mixed
performance over three months but has outperformed on a YTD
basis, suggesting the recent sell-oﬀ has reversed the trend a little
bit.

However, the correction has only moved spreads from tight to
merely quite tight. The AT1 index now trades at a similar level to the
2y median of asw+295bp. Callable Tier 2 also trades just inside the
2y median after the recent widening. The AT1-Callable Tier 2
diﬀerential is around asw+150bp, while the AT1/Callable Tier 2 ratio
is also in line with the 2 year average.

Both remain a bit compressed compared to historical levels,
suggesting that on spread alone, Tier 2 currently oﬀers better
value. We see somewhat greater widening risk in 2027, as bank
fundamentals soften at the margin, while supply rises a bit and
entry valuations remain slightly compressed.
This does not make AT1 necessarily rich at all, but the yield angle
remains the key driver of why AT1 could continue to perform well.
With rates likely to remain elevated, additional yield combined with

some spread widening may lead investors to wait for better entry
points.
Reset has mattered more than ratings
High reset spread AT1s have generally been more resilient in 2026,
with the 700-800bp reset bucket outperforming by quite a bit over
the last three months. While this relationship is not fully linear, and
many high-reset bonds are issued by higher beta/lower rated
issuers, numbers still suggest that reset levels have been an
important driver of performance this year. Higher underlying rates

reduce the cushion that lower reset AT1 bonds have had, as

extension risk concerns have made more of a comeback in recent

Higher reset levels also provide greater coupon protection if a bond
is extended during a period of market stress. There are a few near-
term low-reset bonds callable with reset levels below +380bp.
However, extension risk is still low for these: longer-call AT1s from
these issuers trade through the respective reset levels, and these
banks have historically assessed calls holistically, rather than

purely on an economics or bond-by-bond basis.
Higher-rated bonds have underperformed in 2026. IG-rated bonds
have not translated into better spread performance: BBB- rated
AT1s now trade at similar spread levels as BB+ and BB-rated AT1s
following underperformance this year.

So where does that leave the AT1
market in 2027?
2027 is shaping up to be another interesting year for bank

capital. Valuations look a bit compressed, but bank
fundamentals remain solid, with banks likely to continue
calling their instruments at the ﬁrst call dates. In terms of
supply, we expect the small increase to be absorbable but
potentially lead to investors becoming a bit more price
sensitive. Furthermore, the lack of imminent AT1 reform

台燿 6274 TT ( 電子–電子零組件 )

逆風燿進，屹立不搖 
投資與評價 
1. 台燿 3Q26 在泰國廠進度提前開始營收貢獻下，營運創歷史新高；4Q26 預
期將進行 CCL 產品價格調漲，營運將續創歷史新高。 
2. 台燿 26 年泰國廠區布局順利開出，受益於漲價策略帶動營運將逐季增成
長，27 年有望持續提升高階應用佔比，同時持續擴充產能供應，中長期營
運持續成長，預估 26 年 EPS 41.93 元、27 年 EPS 86.20 元。 
3. 考量台燿受惠高階 AI 應用帶動 CCL 規格持續升級，新產能將持續開出，
26、27 年獲利將持續大幅走升 ，調升 27 年獲利預估，目標價上修至 1,900
元(22x 修正後 27(F)PER)，並將投資評等由買進轉為強力買進。

重點更新 
台燿 3Q26 泰國廠進度提前且轉盈，營運創歷史新高 
台燿 3Q26 受益於泰國廠 1-2 期因學習曲線提升，提前一個月至 7M26 開始貢
獻，爬坡期順利，並且持續開出產能貢獻之下，泰國廠順利轉虧為盈且幅度已超
上半年所有虧損，加上來自美系網通交換器、ASIC 專案持續提升貢獻，帶動台
燿 3Q26 營收季增 25.5%達 179.49 億元，優於原估之 173.16 億元(+21.1% 
QoQ)3.7%。毛利率方面，泰國廠因產能爬坡期順利進行，營運轉虧為盈，帶動
毛利率大幅提升 7.2 個百分點至 36.9%優於原預估 33.3%之 3.6 個百分點，且
因營收規模擴大，再加上銷售費用控管得宜，營業費用率下滑至 5.9%，優於原
預估 6.2%，帶動營業利益達 55.68 億元(+65.8% QoQ)，稅後淨利季增 64.7%
至 38.57 億元，EPS 12.88 元，營運達歷史新高。 
4Q26 CCL 產品價格持續調漲，預期台燿營運將續創新高 
展望 4Q26，泰國廠在產能順開之下，將再度開出10 萬張 CCL 產能，因應來自
終端的強勁需求；另外，目前上游材料包含玻纖布、銅箔等持續進行產品價格調
整，且在高階材料越發緊缺之下，M7/M8 等級之 CCL 產品將較過去有這較大
幅度的調漲，故上修台燿 3Q26 營收預估 6.4%至 207.53 億元(+15.6% QoQ)，
受益於高階 AI 應用業務佔比持續攀升與漲價挹注，毛利率預期將提升 1.6 個百
分點至 38.5%；且因營收規模擴大，營業費用率隨之下滑至 5.8%，帶動營業淨
利達 67.94 億元(+22.0% QoQ)，隨之上調預估稅後淨利 25.3%至 50.99 億元
(+32.2% QoQ)，EPS 17.03 元，營運將續創新高。

並且持續開出產能貢獻之下，泰國廠順利轉虧為盈且幅度已超上半年所有虧損，加上來自美系網
通交換器、ASIC 專案持續提升貢獻，帶動台燿 3Q26 營收季增 25.5%達 179.49 億元，優於原估
之 173.16 億元(+21.1% QoQ)3.7%。

33.3%之 3.6 個百分點，且因營收規模擴大， 再加上銷售費用控管得宜 ， 營業費用率下滑至 5.9%，

億元，EPS 12.88 元，營運達歷史新高。

4Q26 CCL 產品價格持續調漲，預期台燿營運將續創新高 
展望 4Q26，泰國廠在產能順開之下 ， 將再度開出10 萬張 CCL 產能 ， 因應來自終端的強勁需求 ；
另外，目前上游材料包含玻纖布、銅箔等持續進行產品價格調整，且在高階材料越發緊缺之下，
M7/M8 等級之 CCL 產品將較過去有這較大幅度的調漲，故上修台燿 3Q26 營收預估 6.4%至
207.53 億元(+15.6% QoQ)，受益於高階 AI 應用業務佔比持續攀升與漲價挹注，毛利率預期將
提升 1.6 個百分點至 38.5%；且因營收規模擴大，營業費用率隨之下滑至 5.8%，帶動營業淨利
達 67.94 億元(+22.0% QoQ)， 隨之上調預估稅後淨利 25.3%至 50.99 億元(+32.2% QoQ)，EPS 
17.03 元，營運將續創新高。

資料來源: 國泰證期研究部整理

泰國廠區放量貢獻與高階產品提升，預期台燿 26 年營運創新高 
因應眾多 PCB 板廠於泰國廠產能將於 26 年開出，台燿持續擴大於泰國地區之布局。伴隨泰國廠
1-2 期提前至 7M26 上線，4Q26 將進入全面量產階段，專注於衛星地面板、ASIC 伺服器專案
等產品，伴隨產能逐步釋放，26 年底總產能將達 260 萬張，有望進一步帶動台燿於高階 AI 伺服
器、交換器以及衛星相關板材之供應份額。此外，觀察目前上游原料價格走勢持續飆漲，4Q26
仍將持續進行成本轉嫁調漲策略。 
故上修台燿整體營收預估 3.1%至 630.56 億元(+107.8% YoY)。在漲價效益、產品組合優化以及
泰國廠轉營帶動下，調高毛利率預估至 33.9%至(+11.2 ppts YoY)，並且受益於營運規模擴大與
費用控管， 調降營業費用率預估至 6.1%(-2.3 ppts YoY)，營業利益為 175.48 億元(+304.0% YoY)，
帶動稅後淨利達 125.58 億元(+248.6% YoY)，EPS 42.29 元，獲利翻倍成長且營運創新高。

預期台燿 27 年提升更多高階應用占比，營運將再度創高 
展望 27 年，AI 相關需求在更高傳輸速度及訊號低損耗條件下，終端 PCB 規格持續向上靠攏，同
時板層數也持續往 30 甚至 40 層以上研發，帶動整體 CCL 價量齊揚。台燿將受益於 ASIC 客戶
新一代平台的採用，與 AI 伺服器 CPU 板材規格提升至 M8，以及於高階交換器 800G/ 1.6T 新
客戶進展順利下，帶動台燿高階產品組合持續增加。此外，公司亦有望受惠美系衛星供應商 V3
新一代衛星和 800G/ 1.6T 光模塊之採用。來自終端需求遍地開花之下，台燿 27 年亦將持續擴
充產能，預計於中國常熟及泰國各新增 60 萬張，合計120 萬張產能規劃，年底將達 380 萬張總
產能。故上修台燿 27 年營收預估 2.7%至 1,081,29 億元(+71.5% YoY)。 
伴隨台燿持續提升高端應用佔比，再度調高毛利率預估至 40.0%(+6.0 ppts YoY)，且受益於營收
規模持續大幅提升，調低營業費用率至 6.0%(-0.2 ppts YoY)，預期營業利益將達 367.68 億元
(+109.5% YoY)，稅後淨利季增 105.6%至 258.13 億元，EPS 86.20 元，獲利仍將大幅成長且營
運再度創高。

### Page 4

高階產品出貨比重將持續拉高，帶動台燿 26 及 27 年營運續創新高 
台燿 26 年泰國廠區布局順利開出，配合客戶大量終端需求，已於 3Q26 轉虧為盈，加上漲價策
略帶動下營運將季季增，27 年有望持續提升高階應用佔比，如 AI CPU 、ASIC、Switch 等相關
專案，同時持續擴充產能供應，中長期營運持續成長，預期至 27 年獲利仍保持倍增之成長，預
估 26 年 EPS 42.29 元、27 年 EPS 86.20 元。 
目前台燿股價為 1,375 元，約為 15.5x FY27E EPS ，位於近 3 年 PER 參考區間 5~30x 中間偏下
之位置。考量台燿受惠高階 AI 應用帶動 CCL 規格持續升級，且近兩年擴產計畫將順利、穩定開
出貢獻，將有利公司爭取更多淺在訂單，26、27 年獲利將持續大幅走升。近期針對台燿相關 AI
專案之進展，研究部認為下世代平台之規格尚未訂定，公司仍持續與相關客戶密切合作，加上台
燿近兩年擴產計畫迅速且明確，或將有望拿下更多新專案，搶占相關同業份額。故調升 27 年獲
利預估，但考量未來兩季本業成長增速趨緩，將本益比由近上緣之 25 倍略微調低至中間偏上 22
倍，目標價由 1,975 元(25x 修正前 27(F)PER)上修至 1,900 元(22x 修正後 27(F)PER)，並將投資
評等由買進轉為強力買進。

### Page 5

公司簡介 
台燿科技 TUC（6274 TT） 成立於 1974 年，總部位於台灣新竹縣竹北市，初期以光學玻璃製造 
為主要業務 ，1997 年成功轉型跨入銅箔基板 （Copper Clad Laminate, CCL） 與黏合片 （Prepreg） 
之生產領域，2003 年正式在台掛牌上櫃。台燿為全球高階 CCL 材料領導廠之一，核心產品涵蓋 
高頻、高速、低損耗及高耐熱等系列，廣泛應用於AI 伺服器、網通設備、汽車電子、消費性電子 
與高速資料傳輸板材。公司並提供多層壓合代工（Mass Lamination）服務，具備從原材製造到 
加工整合的一站式能力，形成完整的供應鏈垂直整合優勢。3Q26 營收佔比：ELL&SLL(M7/M8) 
38%、VLL&LL(M4/M6) 28%、HDI&N-LL 19%、Others 15%。

台燿 ESG 政策 
台燿科技秉持「誠信經營、創新永續」的企業核心，積極回應全球淨零減碳趨勢。2024 年全面 
導入 ISO 14064-1 溫室氣體盤查制度 ， 完成第三方查證並據此制定短中長期減量目標 ， 以 2021 
年為基準年，規劃至 2025 年減碳 20%、2030 年減碳 42%、2050 年持續每年再降 2%。公
司同 時持續精進節能管理，2024 年推行多項節電專案，節電量達 21 萬度，能源密集度降至 
19.78 (MWh/百萬元營收)，溫室氣體排放總量 108,554 公噸 CO₂e、強度 11.65 。除推動節
能外， 台燿也積極落實廢棄物源頭減量與回收管理，回收率維持 75％ 以上，並推動綠電採購
與碳費應 對策略。公司依 TCFD 架構揭露氣候風險，將氣候議題納入董事會監督範疇，展現與
國際減碳目 標接軌的決心。 台燿推動永續供應鏈管理，強化上游合作夥伴的 ESG 韌性。2024 
年共完成 26 家主要供應商的 環境與人權稽核，合格率 100％，並要求所有供應商簽署社會責
任與環保承諾書，確保不使用衝 突礦產及有害化學物質。公司已通過  RBA 責任商業聯盟稽核
且未有重大缺失，持續推動供應商 進行溫室氣體盤查與減碳培訓，並逐步導入永續採購制度。
為落實環保製造，台燿全廠導入 ISO 14001 環境管理系統與污染防治方案，強化廢水、廢氣與
噪音監控，確保所有放流水與排放皆符 合法規。透過持續改善與外部驗證，台燿建立透明、低碳
且高效率的供應鏈體系，穩固其作為高 階 CCL 材料領導廠的綠色形象。 
在人本管理方面，台燿視員工為企業最重要的資產。公司全面推行 ISO 45001 職業安全衛生管 
理系統，定期舉辦消防演練、健康檢查及安全教育，2024 年無重大職災事件，勞安指標持續改 
善。人權訓練涵蓋新進人員 100％、在職員工 96％，並透過性平與反歧視政策保障同工同酬。 
2024 年非主管職員工薪資平均 NT$951,877，中位數 NT$858,478，較前一年均提升。公司同 
時持續優化福利與人才培育制度，員工人均訓練時數  9.7 小時，女性員工與男性員工訓練時數  
相當。台燿致力打造安全、健康、包容的職場環境，讓員工能在穩定中成長，於工作中實現自我 
價值。 在公司治理層面，台燿以「誠信、透明、問責」為原則，長期維持公司治理評鑑前 6–20
％ 水準。 董事會中獨立董事席次超過半數，並已將 ESG 績效納入高階主管考核機制。公司制
定道德行為 準則與檢舉機制，嚴禁賄賂、貪腐及任何形式的強迫勞動，並推動供應鏈衝突礦產
盡職調查。資 訊安全方面，台燿建置完善的資安防護系統與個資保護管理制度，2024 年未發生
任何資安事件， 員工資安訓練達 691 人次。透過董事會與內控監督機制，台燿持續強化治理透
明度與風險管理 韌性，奠定企業長期穩健經營的基石。 台燿長期深耕在地公益，2024 年投入
社會關懷、教育與環保活動，推動員工志工、節能減碳倡 議及社區回饋計畫。公司定期舉辦淨灘
與捐血活動，並捐助地方教育與弱勢團體，實踐企業公民 責任。未來，台燿將以 「誠信治理、
綠色製造、人本共榮」 為三大支柱，持續朝 2050 淨零排 放目標邁進，推動電子材料產業的永
續創新，致力成為全球客戶信賴的高階 CCL 永續夥伴。

## Document 507: インドネシアGDP（26年7-9月期）～成長率は5.47％に加速、製造業とサービス業が牽引

Source PDF: all report pdf/インドネシアGDP（26年7-9月期）～成長率は5.47％に加速、製造業とサービス業が牽引.pdf
Report source: NLI
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

【結果概要】 
インドネシアの2026年7-9月期実質GDP成長率（速報値）1は前年同期比5.47％となり、前期の
同 5.29％から加速し、Bloomberg調査の市場予想（5.20％）を上回った（図表１）。個人消費が同
5.04％と底堅く推移したほか、政府消費はMBG（無償給食）の関連支出などを背景に同8.95％と高
い伸びを維持したものの、 前期から鈍化した。 総固定資本形成も同6.06％と伸びが鈍化した。 一方、
外需では輸入の伸びが輸出を上回り、純輸出は成長率を下押ししたが、マイナス幅は前期から縮小
した。 
1-9月期の成長率は前年同期比 5.45％となり、政府の 2026年成長率目標である 5.4％を上回っ
た。年末にかけては、政府の追加景気対策が家計消費を下支えするとみられる一方、2027年の成長
加速に向けて民間投資の勢いを強めるかが注目される。

【支出別の詳細】 
支出別にみると、内需は引き続き堅調に推移した。家計最終消費支出は前年同期比5.04％と前期
の同 5.06％からほぼ横ばいとなり、底堅さを維持した（図表２） 。外食需要の拡大に加え、家計の
移動の活発化や公演・スポーツイベントの開催が消費を下支えした。内訳では、外食・宿泊が同
6.59％、交通・通信が同5.83％と比較的高い伸びを維持した一方、食料・飲料は同4.15％、被服・
履物は同3.48％と緩やかな伸びにとどまった。 
政府最終消費支出は同8.95％と前期の同15.97％から減速したものの、引き続き高い伸びとなっ

インドネシア GDP（26年 7-9月期） 
～成長率は 5.47％に加速、製造業とサービス業が牽引

### Page 2

【産業別の詳細】 
第三次産業（サービス業）は前年同期比7.19％と、前期（同6.22％）から加速し、引き続き成長
の中心となった（図表３） 。内訳をみると、構成割合の大きい卸売・小売業（同7.14％）は国内生
産や輸入の増加に伴う商品流通の拡大に加え、自動車販売の回復などを背景に堅調だった（図表４） 。
宿泊・飲食業は同11.22％と、ホテル稼働率の上昇や外食需要の拡大を受けて高い伸びを維持した。
運輸・倉庫業（同8.03％）も道路・海上輸送の拡大を背景に加速したほか、情報・通信（同7.13％）
はデータ通信やデジタルサービスの利用拡大に支えられて堅調だった。 また、 金融・保険 （同8.47％）
は銀行の金融仲介機能の拡大などを受けて大きく加速した。 
第二次産業は前年同期比5.16％となり、前期（同4.08％）から加速した。このうち、GDP全体の
約２割を占める製造業は同5.87％と、前期の同4.52％から伸びが加速した。内訳をみると、食料・
飲料（同7.09％）がパーム油関連製品の国内外の需要拡大を受けて堅調に伸びたほか、化学・医薬
品（同 8.10％）や基礎金属（同 9.94％）もバイオディーゼル原料や非鉄金属などの生産増加を背
景に加速した。一方、金属製品・コンピューター・電子・光学・電気機器（同2.33％）は前期から
減速し、ゴム・プラスチック製品（同▲7.96％）も引き続き減少した。鉱業・採石業は同0.91％と
前期の同▲1.64％からプラスに転じた。金属鉱石の生産が持ち直した一方、石油・ガスや石炭の生

### Page 3

【今後の注目点】 
今後のインドネシア経済では、政府による景気下支えが続くなか、家計消費と民間投資が成長を
支える力を強められるかが注目される。7-9月期は家計消費が底堅く推移した一方、政府消費は前
年同期比 8.95％と前期の同 15.97％から減速した。MBG（無償給食）は前年の対象拡大によるベー
ス効果から、今後も政府消費の伸びを押し上げる効果が弱まる可能性がある。一方、政府は10－12
月期の景気下支え策として、低所得世帯向けのコメ支援を開始したほか、労働者向けの賃金補助も
予定しており、年末にかけて家計の購買力を下支えするとみられる。 
2027年については、政府が予算上の成長率目標を2026年の5.4％から6.0％に引き上げ、積極的
な財政運営と投資拡大による成長加速を目指している。政府は目標達成に向けて投資の伸びを７％
に高める必要があるとしており、政府系投資機関ダナンタラによる戦略産業や資源下流化分野への
投資を進めるとともに、民間投資の拡大を促す方針だ。ただし、7-9月期の総固定資本形成は前年
同期比 6.06％と前期の同 6.87％から減速しており、政府主導の投資が民間の設備投資や雇用・所
得の増加に結びつくかが重要となろう。 
金融面では、インドネシア中銀（BI）が 11月から預金準備率の引き下げを開始し、12月にも追
加の引き下げを予定するなど、銀行貸出を促す方針だ。一方、MSCIは 10月、インドネシア株式市
場を新興国市場から単独市場へ格下げする案を公表した。最終判断は2027年 6月までに下される
予定で、格下げが実現すれば株式市場からの資金流出やルピア安圧力が強まり、追加的な金融緩和
の余地を狭めるおそれがある。また、世界経済や米国の通商政策を巡る不透明感も残る。2027年の
成長率について ADBは 5.2％と予測しており、政府が掲げる 6％成長の実現には民間需要の一段の
拡大が欠かせない。

本資料記載のデータは各種の情報源から入手・加工したものであり、その正確性と完全性を保証するものではありません。 
また、本資料は情報提供が目的であり、記載の意見や予測は、いかなる契約の締結や解約を勧誘するものではありません。

成長率目標の達成が射程圏内に
◆７～９月期のGDPは小幅持ち直し
７～９月期の実質 GDP成長率は 、前年比＋
4.5％と、前期から伸び率が拡大 。１～９月累計
では同＋4.6％と、中国政府が掲げる2026年の成
長率目標の範囲内 。好調な外需が景気を押し上
げ。輸出入額には原油や半導体などの価格上昇の
影響も大きいものの 、実質ベースでは輸入よりも輸
出が伸びており 、GDP成長率に対する純輸出の寄
与度が拡大。
一方、内需は引き続き低迷。とりわけ投資が弱く、
総資本形成のうち固定資産投資は前年比マイナス
幅が拡大。インフラ投資などで持ち直しの動きが見ら
れるものの、過剰生産や不動産不況といった構造的
な下押し要因が残存。
GDPデフレーターは前年比＋ 1.4％と、２期連続
のプラス。もっとも、第二次産業でのエネルギー・原材
料高の価格転嫁が主因であり、内需拡大によるイン
フレとは言い難い状況。
◆成長率目標は達成見込み
10～12月期の成長率は７～９月期を若干上

回り、2026年の成長率目標は達成される見通し。
好調な外需に加え 、追加の内需刺激策が景気を
下支え。「６つのネットワーク」やハイテク製造業といっ
た重点分野向けの金融緩和策が９月末に打ち出
されたほか、住宅ローンの利子補給制度が10月から
開始。もっとも、内需の本格回復には至らず 、景気
の基調としては回復力が弱い状況が続く見通し。

中国
(株)日本総合研究所 中国経済展望 2026年11月3
消費は総じて低調
◆サービス消費は堅調も、財消費が低迷
７～９月期の小売売上高は前年比＋0.7％と、
弱い動き。財消費の低迷が主因であり、なかでも自
動車が大幅減。買い替え補助政策で喚起された需
要の反動減に加え 、原油高でガソリン車需要が減
退。宝飾品も、国際的な金価格の上昇と節約志向
の高まりが相まって、販売数量が大幅減。
先行きの消費も、総じて低調に推移する見通し。
中東危機によるガソリン価格の高止まりなどを背景に、
家計の購買意欲は低迷 。政府は利子補給制度の
対象拡大などで内需を喚起しているものの、大規模
な消費刺激策には慎重な姿勢。一方、サービス消
費は今後の消費拡大の重点分野に位置づけられて
おり、堅調に推移する見込み。
◆住宅市場は長期停滞から脱却できず
住宅市場は引き続き低迷。大都市を中心に住宅
購入要件の緩和などが実施されているものの 、購入
層の減少といった構造的な要因が住宅販売の重石
に。新築住宅価格に加え 、市場の実勢を反映しや

すい中古住宅価格も引き続き下落。在庫の回転期
間も長期化している模様。
こうしたなか、政府は住宅ローンの利子補給制度
を初めて導入 。地方での需要喚起が見込まれるも
のの、実施期間が１年限定であることから政策効果
は一時的にとどまる見通し 。住宅需要の構造的な
弱さも残るため、住宅市場の本格回復にはつながら
ない見込み。

◆投資は下げ止まり
９月の固定資産投資は前年比 ▲7.2％と、２ヶ
月連続でマイナス幅が縮小 。セクター別にみると 、イ
ンフラ投資が底打ち 。 「６つのネットワーク 」（六張
網）の推進により、情報伝送や航空輸送に関連す
る固定資産投資が足元で増加 。製造業も、ハイテ
ク産業の輸出増加がけん引役となってマイナス幅が
縮小。一方、住宅市場の低迷を背景に、不動産開
発投資は大幅減少が継続。
◆年末にかけてマイナス幅が縮小
先行きも、六張網によるインフラ投資の押し上げや、
ハイテク産業での投資拡大が続く見通し 。両者に関
して、政府は金融緩和策を発表。中国人民銀行か
ら政策銀行への貸出である担保付補完貸出
（PSL）の金利を引き下げるとともに 、PSLの支援
対象に六張網を追加 。また、科学技術イノベーショ
ンと技術改造（生産技術の高度化 ）向けの再貸
出限度額を拡大。
もっとも、本格回復は見込めない状況 。製造業や
不動産業では、過剰な生産能力や在庫などの構造

的な下押し要因が残存。製造業の設備稼働率は、
春節の反動で第２四半期に上昇する傾向があるも
のの、本年は第２四半期以降も続落 。ハイテク製
造業などの一部産業を除き、多くの産業で稼働率が
低下。インフラ投資も量的拡大は限定的。財源とな
る地方債は借り換えや隠れ債務の圧縮が優先され 、
重い債務負担が地方政府による投資拡大の制約。

### Page 6

中国
(株)日本総合研究所 中国経済展望 2026年11月5
外需は先行きも堅調
◆外需が頼みの綱も、燻るリスク
実質GDP成長率に対する純輸出の寄与度は拡
大。名目輸出額はAIブームを背景とする半導体な
どの価格上昇で大幅に増加したほか 、７～９月期
は実質ベースでも輸出が輸入を上回る伸び 。米中
関係の改善を受け、対米輸出は前年比プラスで推
移。また、中東危機を契機に世界的な脱炭素化が
一段と加速しており 、EV輸出が拡大。ASEANなど
への中間財輸出も好調を維持しており、先行きも総
じて堅調に推移する見通し。
一方、貿易不均衡の拡大がリスク 。10月、EUが
中国からのハイブリッド車（HV・PHV）に輸入制限
を課す方向で双方合意 。加えて、中国政府は新エ
ネルギー車 （NEV）輸出増値税の還付率を引き
下げ、海外での低価格競争の抑制を図る方針を表
明。こうした動きは摩擦緩和に資するも 、EU側には
さらなる規制強化を求める声 。輸出増値税の変更
を前にした駆け込み輸出がEUや他国の警戒感を煽

る恐れも。輸入規制が幅広い品目や地域に広がれ
ば、外需依存を強める中国経済に打撃。
◆資金流出は拡大傾向
金融収支は流出傾向が継続 。対内直接投資が
減少しているほか、準備資産も縮小。商業銀行など
への外貨資金の振替・預託や資産再配分が進行し
ている可能性。先行きも、内需の低迷が対内投資
の重石となり、金融収支でみた資本流出傾向が継
続する見通し。

・ 2027年の中国製HV・PHVの輸入を35万台とし、
2030年まで毎年６％拡大
… 2030年の輸入枠は42万台になる計算
⇒ 2026年の80万台（見込み）から半減するも、

2025年の水準は維持可能
・ WTOルールに基づくセーフガードの形式をとることで、
EU・中国が合意
中国がNEV輸出増値税の還付率を引き下げ
・ NEV輸出増値税の還付率を13％から９％に変更
・ 2027年４月から適用 ⇒ 駆け込み輸出の恐れ

◆根強いディスインフレ圧力
９月の生産者物価（PPI）は前年比＋5.0％と、
伸びが加速 。原油価格に起因する生産財価格の
再上昇が主因。一方、消費者物価（CPI）は、総
合ベースで同＋0.9％、コアベースで同＋1.0％と、
横ばい圏で推移 。交通燃料のプラス幅が再び拡大
したほか、半導体の価格高騰で通信機器が上昇し
ているものの 、インフレ率は政府目標の２ ％前後を
大きく下回っており 、内需の停滞に起因するディスイ
ンフレ圧力が強い状況。
先行きも、エネルギー価格の高止まりや半導体価
格の上昇を背景に一部財の価格上昇圧力は根強
いものの 、内需の低迷により基調的なインフレ率は
低位で推移する見込み。
◆対米ドルレートは増価基調
10月の人民元の対米ドルレートは増価基調で推
移。中東危機が経済に与える影響や金融政策の
動向を見極める動き市場で続くものの 、貿易不均
衡の是正や資本流出の抑制に向けた人民銀行の
元高容認姿勢もあり、小幅な人民元高圧力が続く

見通し。
◆株価は下落
10月の上海総合株価指数は下落。原材料価格
の上昇率が一服したことを好感して 持ち直す動きが
みられるものの 、中東情勢の不透明感や内需の弱
さが相場の重石に。景気の停滞が続くなか、先行き
も弱い動きが続く見込み。
インフレ率は政府目標に届かず

米中対立の常態化や米国の自国第一主義の強まりを背景に、既存の国際経済秩序が揺らいでいます。こうしたなか、各国は存
在感を高めるグローバルサウス諸国との外交・経済関係の強化を急いでいます。日本も、OECD（経済協力開発機構）などと協調し
ながら、関係強化を目指していますが、現状はASEANやインドなどに偏重しており、アフリカや中南米との連携は限定的です。本シン
ポジウムでは、はじめに世界経済の構造変化や日本のグローバルサウス諸国との連携策の現状・課題を多角的に検証します。その
上で、日本がグローバルサウス諸国との関係を深化させるための具体的な戦略を議論します。

## Document 577: 債券市場總評：非投等高息有撐

Source PDF: all report pdf/債券市場總評：非投等高息有撐.pdf
Report source: Fubon
Date: 2026-10-05
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

台北富邦銀行 
Oct 5，2026 | 3:00 PM (GMT+8) 
債券市場總評： 非投等高息有撐
投資研究部 
 Research 
Financial Market 
▎美國經濟具韌性，支撐年底升息預期 
9 月下旬以來，美債殖利率快速上行，主要因經濟數
據仍具韌性，加上中東情勢反覆推升油價與通膨疑
慮，牽動市場持續評估後續升息空間。雖然 8 月 PCE
與核心 PCE 低於預期，但先前公布的 9 月製造業與服
務業 PMI 大幅優於預期，第 2 季 GDP 終值上修，顯
示經濟動能仍強。但最新 9 月非農意外疲弱，新增就
業僅 2.9 萬人、失業率升至 4.2%，降低短期升息急迫
性，10 月升息機率降溫，但 12 月升息預期仍存，市
場轉向觀望 9 月 CPI 公布，10 年期美債殖利率短暫回
落後仍處高檔。 
圖 1 就業數據降溫，惟美債殖利率仍處高檔

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10/5 
▎殖利率攀升至高位，債市同步下滑 
近日部分 Fed 官員表示，後續利率決策仍需觀察更多
數據，略為緩和市場對進一步升息的定價，帶動 10 年
期美債殖利率自高點小幅回落至 5.25%。不過，殖利
率與油價仍處高檔，限制債市修復空間。9 月下旬以
來，主要債券 YTD 報酬同步下滑，美國公債與投資級
債跌幅較深，非投等債則因存續期較短、風險偏好尚
穩，跌幅相對較輕。

圖 2 利率與油價高檔，主要債券報酬下滑

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10/5 
◼ 近期美國數據仍具韌性 ， 中東局勢推升油價與通膨疑慮 ，PCE 雖低於預期 ，但 PMI
與 GDP 仍強，非農轉弱緩和升息預期，美債殖利率仍處高檔，債市承壓。美非投
等債受高息收與中短存續期緩衝 ， 跌幅較輕 ， 殖利率升至8.3%， 處近10 年高位。 
◼ 美非投等債信用結構較過去改善，較高評級的 BB 級占比已逾半，違約率約 2%，
位於近 25 年低檔。美非投等企業 2Q 營收與 EBITDA 分別年增 10.4%、14.4%，
增速加快並創近 4 年較強表現 ， 獲利增速快於營收 ， 也帶動槓桿與償息指標改善。 
◼ 殖利率高檔震盪下，債券報酬較難仰賴價格貢獻，息收成為更重要的收益來源。
美非投等債因票息較高、存續期較短，可提供一定報酬緩衝。後續可依風險承受
度，關注科技、金屬與礦業、能源等基本面改善較明顯產業，並偏向 BB、短中天
期及現金流較穩定發行公司。

2026 年初至今報酬率 
美國非投等債報酬率 0.14% 
美國投等債報酬率 -2.74% 
美國公債報酬率 -3.13%

美國 10 年公債殖利率 5.25% 
西德州原油期貨 $90/桶 
美國 10 年期公債殖利率 5.25%

2

誠信親切、專業創新 週報 
▎高息收提供緩衝，支撐非投等債韌性 
近期公債殖利率快速上行 ， 推升企業債殖利率至近年相
對高位，其中美國非投資等級債到期殖利率已來到約
8.3%，顯著高於過去 10 年平均，殖利率水準已具備相
當吸引力。 
圖 3 美非投等債殖利率攀升至 8.3%，10 年相對高檔

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10/1

觀察近 6 個月各類債券報酬來源 ， 今年美國公債殖利率
攀升，使債券報酬較難仰賴價格貢獻，息收重新成為影
響總報酬的關鍵來源。其中，美國非投資等級債因具備
較高殖利率與中短存續期，且息收水準相對較高，較能
緩衝利率上行對價格的影響， 因此相較公債與投等債，
回落幅度相對有限。

圖 4 利息收益貢獻為債券報酬主要來源

資料來源：JPM，2026/9/30

▎美經濟相對穩健 ， 非投等BB 比重提升 
從已開發國家 GDP 年增率來看，美國、歐元區與日本
未來幾季仍維持正成長， 其中美國成長表現相對穩健 ，
預估仍維持約 2%左右增速 ， 顯示高利率與油價波動下 ，
經濟仍具一定韌性。

圖 5 美國經濟動能仍相對歐元區、日本較具韌性

資料來源：JPM，2026/9/26

信用結構方面， 美國非投等債市場債信結構已較金融海
嘯前改善，BB 評級占比由 2007 年 12 月的 32%升至
2026 年 5 月的 54%，目前占整體非投等債比重過半，
CCC 與未評級占比則由 15%降至 7%。 由於BB 是非投
等債中評級最高的一層，信用風險低於 B 與 CCC，BB
級占比提高代表目前美國非投等債市場中 ，信用體質較
接近投資等級的發行公司占比明顯提升。 
圖 6 美國非投等債各信用評級占比與違約率

資料來源：JPM，2026/8/31

%

美國投等債殖利率 6.01%

10 年平均 3.97%

10 年平均 6.62%

3

誠信親切、專業創新 週報 
違約率方面，目前美國非投等債整體違約率約 2%，落
在歷史區間相對低位 ， 低評級債違約壓力也未見明顯擴
散。在美國經濟仍具韌性、非投等債 BB 比重提升且違
約率受控的背景下 ， 美國非投等債的高殖利率仍具基本
面支撐。

▎美非投等企業，2Q 營收獲利顯著改善 
檢視美國非投等債企業第 2 季基本面，營收與 EBITDA
延續正成長動能，且2Q26 成長幅度進一步加速。美國
非投等債企業營收季增 9.2%、 年增10.4%，EBITDA 季
增 19.2%、 年增14.4%， 營收與EBITDA 年增率雙雙創
下 4 年來最高，顯示企業營運表現仍具韌性。

圖 7  美非投等企業營收年增 10.4%，4 年來最快增速

資料來源：JPM，2026/9/15

從產業表現來看，今年商品、能源與企業投資需求仍延
續，金屬與礦業、能源、科技、零售與服務業等產業對
整體營收與 EBITDA 成長形成貢獻，使 2Q 基本面改善
具備一定廣度。EBITDA 增速高於營收增速，代表企業
獲利擴張速度快於收入成長，反映成本控管、營運效率
或價格轉嫁能力仍在。 
圖 8 EBITDA 年增顯著提升至 14%，4 年來較強增速

資料來源：JPM，2026/9/15 
▎美非投等槓桿回落，償息能力改善 
2Q 美國非投等債企業信用指標出現改善，槓桿率降至 
4.51 倍 ， 主要反映EBITDA 成長速度快於債務增加 ， 帶
動槓桿指標修復。

圖 9 美非投等企業槓桿率回落至 4.51 倍

資料來源：JPM，2026/9/15

償息能力方面，儘管利息費用仍在增加，但 EBITDA 成
長仍帶動利息保障倍數回升至 4.12 倍，2 年較高水準 ，
顯示企業獲利對利息支出的覆蓋能力有所改善 ， 高利率
環境下，美非投等企業的償息能力仍相對有撐。整體來
看，2Q26 美國非投等債企業營收、獲利與信用指標同
步改善，基本面韌性仍在。

圖 10 美非投等企業利息保障倍數升至 2 年高位

資料來源：JPM，2026/9/15

### Page 4

4

誠信親切、專業創新 週報 
▎非投等具高息收優勢 ， 關注BB 短天期 
近期公債殖利率仍在高檔波動 ， 美非投等債短線表現受
到壓抑。不過，經過利率重新定價後，美非投等債殖利
率已回到相對具吸引力的水準 ， 較高息收與相對較短存
續期，仍可在利率波動環境下提供一定報酬緩衝。 
綜合前述，美國經濟相對歐日仍較穩健，企業營收、獲
利與信用指標同步改善 ， 為美非投等債後續表現提供基
本面基礎。產業面可留意科技、基礎工業相關的金屬與
礦業、能源等基本面改善較明顯的方向。整體而言，後
續可偏向 BB、短中天期及現金流較穩定的發行公司，
參與較高息收帶來的收益機會。

### Page 5

美国名义中性利率为4.3%，9月初基准利率为3.5-3.75%，理论上存在2--3次的加息空间

### Page 6

美联储6月声明中取消了利率指引
数据来源：WIND，格林大华期货
在提供利率预测的18名联储官员中，共有九人预计，到今年底，联储将至少有一次25个基点的加息。
点阵图显示，预测今年加息的官员中，预计一次加息的有三人，预计两次加息的有五人，预计三次加息的有
一人。
九人中共六人预计至少两次加息，人数占提供利率预测总人数的三分之一。
本次声明的全文字数缩减将近三分之二。同时，本次改变了声明的叙事结构，删除了此前重申的所谓利率指
引，不再称FOMC将根据未来数据对经济前景的影响评估货币政策的立场。
在双重使命方面，本次声明不再继续重申，FOMC致力于在长期内实现充分就业和2%的通胀率目标，也不再
重申，FOMC密切关注在就业和通胀两方面面临的风险，而是在介绍经济形势之后，即全文的末尾，加了一
句表述“（FOMC）委员会将致力于实现价格稳定”，以此强调了通胀方面的使命。
本次未公布改变影响资产负债表的购买国债相关行动，但美联储调整了政策实施说明的措辞，反映出，
FOMC指示纽约联储的公开市场交易台“在适当时”增加购买短期美国国债。

### Page 7

沃什的镜厅概念 与 市场更加剧烈的波动性
数据来源：美联储，格林大华期货
沃什的镜厅概念：如果市场严重依赖美联储的指导，而美联储又依赖市场价格，我们都更可能对新事态视而
不见，更可能在事态转折时措手不及，更可能在政策制定中犯错。
在正常时期，前瞻性指引的作用应是有限和受约束的。否则，它就有以清晰之名制造模糊的风险。过度分享
政策审议和过度承诺未来决策，可能会误导市场、企业和家庭。
市场参与者将始终试图预测我们下一步的行动。但我们不应纵容一个市场参与者主要依赖美联储来决定其下
一次交易的体制。一个更安静、沟通更有针对性的美联储，更能够实现其目标。
要制定正确的政策，我们还需要处理好金融市场与央行之间的关系。美联储需要清晰的市场信号，尽可能未
经过滤。来自市场内部、各类资产价格的水平与变化、国债的价格和交易量、美元的汇价、信贷的成本和可
用性、以及广泛大宗商品的价格。
点评：美联储转向依靠市场信号进行政策判断，而市场信号的形成严重依赖政府部门公布的经济数据，包括
CPI、PCE、PPI。而美国政府公布的通胀数据、就业数据存在很强的政治倾向性，并经常在日后大幅度修正。

2年期国债收益率决定美联储政策方向，市场利率决定政策利率，大类资产市场趋向于更加剧烈的波动

### Page 8

沃什G20放鹰，美国实际政策利率已转换为2年期国债收益率
数据来源：美联储，格林大华期货
8月28日晚，Jackson Hole会议上，沃什清楚地表示：通货膨胀太高，虽然有所下降，但还不够低；通胀已
经持续了65个月，责任完全在美联储；美联储要坚持自己的使命，压低通胀，政策工具就是短期利率。
沃什的主张是：坚持两大使命；加息、缩表、抗通胀，坚持美联储的独立性，厘清美联储与财政部之间界限。
8月31日，沃什在G20财长与央行行长会议上致辞上说：“我此前说过，通胀是一种选择。今天我要补充的
是——增长，也是一种选择。
沃什称：新时代是长期增长（secular growth）的时代。如果要我描述这一时刻，那就是全球投资浪潮。如
果让我来概括当下的局势，那就是全球投资激增（global investment surge）。
沃什认为，廉价资金时代已经结束，经济增长正在加速，资金正在涌向新的项目，这将推高利率，而不是降
低利率。
美联储基准利率成为吉祥物，2年期美债收益率已演化为美联储实际政策利率。

### Page 9

美联储6月会议纪要显示，AI基建成为推动通胀的重要变量
数据来源：WIND，格林大华期货
美联储6月会议纪要显示，官员们普遍认为，如果今年通胀持续处于高位，就需要进一步提高利率。
多位与会者表示，价格压力已经变得更加广泛，许多商品和服务价格均出现了明显上涨。
越来越多的官员认为，AI投资热潮可能成为通胀持续维持高位的重要推动因素。
更多官员首次将AI基础设施建设带来的企业投资热潮列为持续通胀的新来源。
多位官员指出，大规模建设数据中心、持续扩充算力基础设施，使美国经济面临新的需求冲击，而供给能力
正难以跟上。
就业市场已经趋于稳定，而能源价格上涨与AI投资热潮又同时推高成本，这意味着继续“等待观察”可能增
加通胀长期高于目标的风险。
关税、油价以及AI投资热潮形成了一轮又一轮叠加的价格冲击，不断考验美联储“忽略一次性价格上涨”的
政策框架。

### Page 10

美联储7月议息会议：沃什--- 长债收益率上行是有效的紧缩传导
数据来源：华为，格林大华期货
美联储7月29日会议决定维持联邦基金利率目标区间在3.5%至3.75%不变，投票结果为9比3。
三位委员（Hammack、Kashkari、Logan）反对，主张上调25个基点。声明措辞从"重申"改为"继续执行"，
其余表述与6月基本一致。
本次声明再次重申，中东冲突导致经济高度不确定性，通胀仍处高位、部分源于能源价格上涨，经济稳步扩
张、失业率基本保持不变。
美联储减少前瞻指引的目的是获取市场“未经过滤”的信号，让国债价格、美元汇率等由买卖双方自由交易
形成，美联储再据此判断通胀与就业状况。
“市场参与者正在学习跟着球走，而不是跟着裁判走，市场价格将继续朝着他们认为合适的方向和幅度作出
反应。在我看来，这是向好的变化”。
自6月17日会议以来，市场对事件的反应更加直接，名义利率和实际利率均大幅上升，这本身就是一种有效的
紧缩传导，不应被央行的预测或评论所干扰。
AI相关投资正在推升高科技资本开支，但其对生产率、供给能力和通胀的最终影响尚难准确判断。这意味着，

投资与生产率改善能否缓解价格压力，仍是美联储后续政策评估中的关键变量。

### Page 11

美国财政部进行买长债、卖短债操作，抑制长债收益率
数据来源：WIND，格林大华期货
8月19日周三，美国财政部宣布将大幅扩大国债回购规模，计划将10年至30年期国债单次回购上限从20亿美
元至少翻倍至40亿美元。9月9日起生效，至少持续至11月4日。
美国长债收益率应声回落，30年期国债收益率在数小时内下跌近10个基点。但效用短暂，周四开盘时，10年
期美债收益率已经完全收复周三的跌幅，30年期美债收益率也收复近半跌幅。
美国财政部的措施为买长卖短，即回购长久期国债，同时发行更多两年期国债。
此举也传递出一个信息，即政府可以采取更多措施来控制收益率。
市场人士对此举的持续效果持怀疑态度，认为在财政赤字高企、通胀顽固的背景下，操作层面的干预难以从
根本上压制收益率。
8月20日美国财长贝森特谈美债回购，称：这是一个交投清淡的市场板块。我们在国债市场上“工具箱充足”，
对国债市场有一个强大的工具箱。单次回购规模可能超过我们宣布的40亿美元。30年期美债的流动性尤其匮
乏。我们可以“通过自身发展”来摆脱债务负担。

### Page 12

随着买盘的大规模涌入，美国长债价格快速回升，对全球资金的虹吸效应边际减弱
数据来源：文华财经，格林大华期货

### Page 13

10月7日，390亿美元10年期美债认购倍数达2.77倍，创10年来新高；非交易商投资者认购比例创历
史纪录97.5%，间接投标人获配80.3%、为历史第四高，显示外资需求异常强劲。5%收益率已具有极
强的吸引力。
数据来源：文华财经，格林大华期货

### Page 14

全球最大资管贝莱德10月转向 看多短期美债
全球最大资产管理公司贝莱德（BlackRock）宣布对美国国债和股票的核心配置立场作出重大调整，标志着其
坚守近六年的低配美债策略正式终结。
贝莱德投资研究院将短期美国国债上调至超配，将长期美债由低配上调至中性，理由是当前收益率水平已具
吸引力，且市场对美联储加息的预期可能“过度”。
贝莱德策略师：如今收益率已回归我们长期以来预判的高利率水平，希望锁定部分具有吸引力的收益。
贝莱德团队表示，在当前收益率水平下，即便未来12个月内收益率再上行75个基点，美债所产生的利息收入
仍足以抵消价格损失，总回报不至于转负。这一测算为其超配短期美债提供了量化支撑。
贝莱德认为市场当前对美联储加息的定价已走得过远。利率互换数据显示，交易员预计未来12个月内美联储
将至少加息三次，每次25个基点。贝莱德认为，一旦这些押注被修正，短期美债将尤为受益。
贝莱德此次转向，是大型资产管理机构在收益率大幅上行后集体重新审视固定收益配置的缩影。

数据来源：WIND，格林大华期货

1、美国不是一个过热的经济。
2、没有看到除了人工智能和能源之外的广泛价格压力。
3、没有迹象表明劳动力市场正在推动通胀。
4、抑制通胀的力量正在发挥作用。
5、年内再加息一次后，预计2027年无需进一步加息。
6、当前距离美国中性利率“已不远”。AI短期存在供给缺口，长期来看，产能扩张以及生产率提升“会成为
一股反通胀的力量。
7、美国是超过30万亿美元的庞大经济体，货币政策和金融条件收紧正在给其他领域降温。

### Page 19

AI债券大规模发行，推升美债收益率
数据来源：华为，格林大华期货
今年以来美国投资级企业债发行量已同比增长38%，全年有望创下2.1万亿美元的历史纪录，其中大量资金流
向AI相关资本支出。
AI基础设施军备竞赛已成为本轮企业债发行热潮的核心驱动力。微软、Alphabet、亚马逊、Meta和Oracle自
去年秋季以来持续大规模发行长期企业债，为数据中心、先进芯片及AI服务投入提供资金。
截至7月下旬，2026年AI相关债务（含投资级、高收益及杠杆贷款市场）总量已接近5000亿美元。
庞大的AI企业债供给正在与长期国债形成直接竞争。AI企业债通常期限较长，发行主体的信用评级有时甚至
高于美国联邦政府，这对长期国债构成替代效应。
科技公司正在开拓表外融资渠道，包括针对特定数据中心项目的大型融资以及芯片抵押融资等创新结构。
用"债券洪水"来形容当前局面——每天都有新的交易涌现，来源不限于超大规模云计算企业本身，还涵盖AI
产业链上的各类公司。
据英国金融时报，AI行业融资越来越依赖债务，2026年AI相关债务发行规模可能超过6000亿美元，达到

2024年的约10倍。随着全球利率水平上升，资本成本正在成为AI基础设施投资无法回避的约束。

49%至50%的固定汇率增速。
本季度Azure强劲表现背后，效率改善是关键变量。工程团队在GPU和CPU层面持续挖掘效率空间，使微软得
以将原本受供给约束的算力更多分配至Foundry平台。
本季度token定价高于预期，高质量模型带来了更高的每token收益，对季度业绩形成额外贡献。
客户的需求继续超过了我们可用的供应。
企业客户的AI部署正从试验阶段转向生产部署，关注点已从"下一个技术亮点"转向"能否构建稳定系统、掌控
模型权重、管控成本和保障安全"。
企业级业务不是每个季度看一次的东西。这是一个长达十年的过程。
本季度资本支出达452亿美元，部分第三方算力交付延迟至下半年。预计下季度资本开支将超过500亿美元，
全年合计约1700亿美元，下半年将进一步加速。
数据来源：微软，格林大华期货

谷歌电话会：云收入劲增103%，云业务积压订单高达5610亿美元，

云业务积压订单高达5610亿美元，TPU收入大规模确认将在2027年到来。
就像十年前我们转向移动端一样，我们专注于先打造最佳搜索用户体验，然后再实现变现。
自由现金流转负主要受到TPU库存前置的“时间差”影响，TPU收入将在2027年放量，2027年是收获之年。
由于供给受限，谷歌已在Q3末开始启用第三方计算容量，Q4将进一步加大使用。
TPU系统不再只在谷歌云上提供，现在已可部署在客户自己的数据中心。
2027年资本开支也将“显著增加”。谷歌母公司Alphabet预计全年资本开支1950亿-2050亿美元。
在整个投资周期中，我们预计将产生有吸引力的投入资本回报率，且将显著高于我们的资本成本，并预计将
产生强劲的运营现金流。

元，较上年同期的114.34亿美元增长约55%。

Trainium芯片。

美元。过去12个月，公司购置物业和设备净额达到2106.6亿美元，同比增长82%。

可观的财务回报”。

新云服务商CoreWeave的债券收益率突破15%

### Page 31

CoreWeave债券波动不会放缓AI基建增速
数据来源：CoreWeave，格林大华期货
CoreWeave是新兴云服务商，缺乏传统业务现金流支持，主要依靠发债募资，其本次2031年到期的债券收益
率达到15%，发行时债券收益率即高达9.6%，票面利率为9%。
算力租赁价格在继续上升，云服务商的算力服务盈利极为强劲，当前算力基础设施投入一年即可收回成本。
市场担忧的是CoreWeave从算力基础设施投入到获得算力收入，用时过长，现金流能否覆盖利息与资本开支。
划重点：100%的利润  VS 15%的融资成本，哪个更高呢？
CoreWeave公司CEO 表示，称公司拥有逾1042亿美元的订单积压，完全足以支撑所有债务义务。
英伟达CEO黄仁勋出面“灭火”，称CoreWeave财务实力持续改善、融资成本正在下降。一锤定音。
CoreWeave联合创始人Brannin McBee日前接受《金融时报》采访时表示，公司希望获得投资级信用评级，
从而打开更广泛的融资渠道，甚至能够借入“无限”规模的资金池。

退一步讲，即使CoreWeave无法再融资，也只是拱手将快速增长的算力市场让给其它云服务商，而并非意味
着算力市场会出现衰退。单家公司的信用风险和指数级增长的算力需求是两个不同概念。AI基建不会放缓。

### Page 32

野村：AI科技公司的利润和现金流，2028年之后现金流有望转正
数据来源：野村，格林大华期货

### Page 33

SemiAnalysis：2028年全球算力将达239GW
数据来源：野村，格林大华期货

### Page 34

SemiAnalysis创始人：算力收益狂飙，两大巨头将垄断全球绝大部分可用算力，推升全球发债利率
数据来源：SemiAnalysis，格林大华期货
SemiAnalysis创始人Dylan Patel预计，2028年OpenAI与Anthropic两大巨头将垄断全球绝大部分可用算力。
今年新增上线算力中约三分之一被 OpenAI 和 Anthropic 瓜分，到 2028 年，两家头部实验室预计将拿下全
球 70%至80% 的增量算力，单家算力规模将超过 50 吉瓦（GW）。更为关键的是性能乘数效应：今年部署
的每一瓦特算力，其搭载的 GB300、TPUv7 或 Trainium3，在每瓦性能上比上一代芯片高出 3 到 5 倍。当
这两家实验室吃下 2028 年全球 70%至80% 的新增算力时，由于这批算力具有最高的能效比，它们实际上垄
断了全世界绝大部分可用的有效计算能力。
算力收益狂飙：算力的基础成本通常在 1000 万至 1500 万美元/兆瓦，而Anthropic 的算力产出已达每兆瓦

5000 万美元，全公司综合收入向 7000 万至 8000 万美元/兆瓦进发，并正向 1 亿美元迈进，这支撑它们以
每兆瓦 2500 万至 5000 万美元的天价向市场扫货。
AI算力的惊人暴利正掀起投资狂潮。2024 至 2029 年全球 AI 相关资本开支预计达 11 万亿美元，其中需通过
信贷市场举债超 5 万亿美元。
由于 AI 数据中心展现出惊人的投资回报率，科技巨头愿意承受 8% 甚至更高的发债成本，对资本的极端争夺
将推高全社会的折现率与发债利率，甚至引发全球借贷成本的系统性上升。

## Document 616: 凱基投顧_測試介面產業_許芝瑄_20261104

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_測試介面產業_許芝瑄_20261104.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十一月 04, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  1

產業報告  台灣

測試介面產業

TPU 供應鏈漸趨明朗，測試板業者成長動能各有消長  
重要訊息 
TPU 測試介面供應鏈重新分工，精測( 6510 TT, NT$ 2,770, 增加持股)因供應
範圍縮減而下修獲利預估，雍智( 6683 TT, NT$942, 增加持股)則受惠高規格
SLT 測試板訂單及產品組合轉佳，帶動獲利上修。儘管 TPU 供應份額有所調
整，兩家公司仍持續受惠於高階測試需求成長，長期成長趨勢不變。  
評論與分析 
下一世代 TPU 測試介面供應鏈重新分工 。台系 IC 設計業者的 TPU 專案，本
世代各類測試板均由精測供應，惟下一世 代供應商配置將有所調整。根據我
們最新的供應鏈調查，下一代 TPU 的 CP 探針卡將由領導性晶圓代工廠提
供，先前市場預期雍智有望切入完整探針卡供應，最終並未實現，然雍智 仍
有機會供應部分探針卡 PCB。後段測試方面，FT 測試板將持續由精測供
應，SLT 測試板則由雍智負責。BIB 供應商配置尚未確定，惟考量本世代
burn- in 測試遭遇多項問題，我們預期下一代雍智有望承接部分訂單。  
精測：TPU 份額下滑拖累獲利預期，車用與 CPU 探針卡增添成長動能 。隨
下一代 TPU 供應鏈分工逐步確立，精測將由本世代全面供應各類測試板，
轉為以 FT 測試板為主，供應份額下滑已成定局。儘管 TPU 需求成長仍可支
撐相關業務，供應範圍縮減將使精測受惠程度低於原先預期， 我們因此下修
2027 年獲利預估 11.5%。此外，市場近期傳出精測於 AI GPU 的供應份額
將由目前約 70%降至 50%，我們認為此訊息屬實，然 主要 GPU 客戶採雙
供應商策略，並以雙方整體供應份額各約 50%為配置方向，因此單一產品
的份額增減，通常伴隨其他產品的相反調整。精測於部分產品的份額雖將下
降，仍可由其他產品的供應配置抵銷部分影響，我們認為對整體營運的衝擊
有限。同時， 精測在探針卡業務持續拓展應用， 除車用大廠外 ，CPU 領域
亦有望切入。其中，CPU 潛在業務尚未納入財務模型，若後續順利取得訂
單，將提供獲利預估上修空間。綜合 TPU 供應份額調整及其他產品成長，
我們預估 2027-28 年 EPS 分別為 106.28 元及 146.56 元，年增 73.0%及
37.9%。 
雍智：SLT 測試板足以彌補 TPU 探針卡未能切入的營收缺口 。雖然雍智未
能取得下一代 TPU 完整探針卡訂單，但取得 SLT 測試板業務。下一代 SLT
測試板規格大幅升級，帶動 ASP 呈倍數增長，加上預期出貨數量可觀，我
們認為相關營收貢獻足以完全填補原先預估的 TPU 探針卡營收，因此維持
2027- 28 年營收預估不變。雍智另有機會供應部分探針卡 PCB 及 BIB，後續
訂單配置仍值得追蹤。相較於完整探針卡須外購探針、墊高成本， SLT 測試
板毛利率較高。因此，儘管營收規模預估不變，成長來源由完整探針卡轉向
SLT 測試板，仍有利於整體毛利率及獲利表現。我們據此上修 2027-28 年獲
利預估 3.1%及 5.5%，預估 EPS 分別為 41.66 元及 75.92 元，年增 69.4%
及 82.2%。 
投資建議 
精測因供應範圍縮減而下修獲利預估，但仍保有 FT 業務，並具車用及 CPU
探針卡拓展機會；雍智則受惠 SLT 測試板規格與單價提升，在維持營收預估
的同時，透過較佳產品組合推升獲利。我們對兩家公司均維持「增加持股」
投資評等，目標價皆以 2028 年預估 EPS 的 30 倍本益比計算，精測為
4,380 元，雍智為 2,280 元。 
投資風險 
大型雲端業者資本支出放緩 ；全球經濟衰退。 
焦點內容

1. 下一世代 TPU 測試介面供應鏈重新分
工。 
2. 精測：TPU 份額下滑拖累獲利預期，
車用與 CPU 探針卡增添成長動能。  
3. 雍智：SLT 測試板足以彌補 TPU 探針
卡未能切入的營收缺口。

## Document 653: 基調的物価上昇率２％到達後の金融政策

Source PDF: all report pdf/基調的物価上昇率２％到達後の金融政策.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 23 日(金)

1/3 
基調的物価上昇率２％到達後の金融政策  
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・日経新聞は10月22日に「日銀10月会合、連続利上げ見送りへ ２％物価目標は事実上到達」
と題する記事で10月29～30日の金融政策決定会合で政策金利を据え置く方針であることを報
じた。展望レポートでは、基調的な物価上昇率が２％に到達したことを記述するという。

・日銀は、これまで基調的な物価上昇率の２％到達時期を「2026年度後半から2027年度にかけ
て」と予想してきた。したがって、2026年度後半に突入するこの時期に２％到達の認識を示
すことに違和感はない。とはいえ、これまで「表面的な消費者物価上昇率は２％を超えてい
ても、基調的な物価上昇率は２％に到達していない」という認識が金融緩和度合いの調節
（利上げ）をゆっくり進めることの理論的根拠となってきた以上、そうした認識変化は政策
の局面が変わることを改めて印象付ける。

・その上で10月会合の注目点を整理する。筆者は①米ＦＯＭＣの結果、②高田委員と田村委員
の投票行動、③浅田委員と佐藤委員の物価認識、④植田総裁の記者会見が重要と考える。

・①10月ＦＯＭＣは利上げ見送りが中心的な予想となっており、筆者も見送りを予想する。Ｆ
Ｆ金利先物から逆算した利上げ確率は７％程度と低水準にあり、さすがのウォーシュ議長も
・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000 円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPY は先行き12 ヶ月 155 円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12 月に 1.5％とした後、27 年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FED はFF 金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

### Page 2

2/3 
サプライズ利上げは選択しないだろう。９月ＦＯＭＣ後にジェファーソン副議長、ウォラー
理事、ウィリアムズ・ＮＹ連銀総裁など中枢メンバーが連続利上げに積極的でない発言をし
ていることを踏まえれば、少なくとも12月までは利上げを待つと考えられる。なお、仮にＦ
ｅｄがサプライズ利上げをした場合でも、日銀は様子見を選択すると予想される。日米金利
差の拡大によって円安が進む可能性は否定できないが、現在の為替水準から判断すると、慌
てて追随利上げする必要性は乏しいだろう。

・②利上げ見送りに対して、高田委員と田村委員が反対票を投じるか否かも注目したい。高田
委員は、政策金利の据え置きに対して１月、３月、４月、７月の４回の会合で反対票を投じ
ており、今回も利上げを主張するとみられる。田村委員は４月の据え置きに際しては利上げ
を主張して反対票を投じたものの、７月は賛成票を投じており、今回どう動くかは興味深
い。仮に田村委員も利上げを主張するならば、次回以降の利上げ観測を強める要因になろ
う。一方で、声明文等における「物価の基調判断」に対する反対票は取り下げる公算が大き
い。

・③基調的な物価上昇率が２％に到達したとの認識に対して、浅田委員と佐藤委員が反対票を
投じる可能性はある。両委員は、日本経済の足腰の弱さを理由に９月会合で利上げに反対票
を投じた経緯があり、今回もハト派色を前面に押し出す可能性がある。仮に反対票が投じら
れれば、円安を誘発し得る。

・最後に、植田総裁の記者会見はいつもながら言質を与えずに終わると予想されるが、３～４
ヶ月に一度の利上げが標準になりつつあることは確認できるだろう。すなわち、今回の利上
げ見送りは、飽くまで時間調整を目的とするもので、連続利上げを選択しなくてはいけない
ほど物価の基調が上振れているわけではない、との認識を示すだろう。間接的に12月ないし
は１月の利上げを示唆すると考えられる。

・なお、金融政策を読むうえで重要となる2027年春闘が10月22日に実質的に開幕した。連合は
要求賃上げ率を「６％中心、５％以上」、ベア相当部分を「４％中心、３％以上」にすると
した。定期昇給を含んだ数値は、2026年春闘で掲げた「５％以上」よりも表現を強め、目標
を高めた格好となっている。ここから判断すると、日銀が重視している毎月勤労統計（共通
事業所版）における一般労働者の所定内給与の上昇率は、現在の３％弱から加速する可能性

### Page 3

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
3/3
がある。毎月勤労統計で観測される賃金上昇率は、過去数年、製造業の存在感が強い春闘賃
上げ率の数値（2026年はベア相当分が3.5％）ほどには高まらない傾向があることを勘案する
必要はあるが、利上げが続く可能性を高めると判断される。

## Document 710: 時評『「周回遅れ」からの出発』

Source PDF: all report pdf/時評『「周回遅れ」からの出発』.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

「周回遅れ」 からの出発
法政大学経済学部教授　菅 幹雄（すが みきお）
筆者が大学生だった1990年頃、 授業では 「日本の統
計は世界一正確だ。 アメリカの統計などはかなりいいか
げんだ」 と教えられていた。 当時の私はそれを素直に受
け止め、 日本の統計制度を誇らしく思ったものである。
それから約10年後の2000年頃、 大学教員となった私
は、 後に 「経済センサス」 （いわゆる企業版国勢調査） の
創設につながるプロジェ ク トの一環と して、 アメリカ ・ セ
ンサス局を訪問し、 産業統計の仕組みについてインタ
ビューする機会を得た。 そこで目の当たりにしたのは、 大
学時代に教えられていたこととはまったく逆の現実だっ
た。 産業統計の制度では、 アメリカの方が日本よりはる
か先を走っていたのである。
戦後日本の統計制度がアメリカの強い影響を受けて
形成されたことはよく知られている。 ライス統計使節団
が来日し、 統計制度の再建に貢献し、 現在まで続く多く
の基幹的な統計調査も占領期に始まった。 そのため、 私

たちも 「日米の統計制度は基本的には似たものだろう」
と漠然と考えていた。
しかし、 アメリカでは1950年代以降、 産業統計の仕組
みが大きく変わり、 その後も改革が積み重ねられてい
た。 行政記録の積極的な利用、 ビジネスレジスターの構
築、 供給側の産業活動を的確に把握する産業分類、 企業
組織構造の把握、 産業の実態に応じた数百種類もの調
査票――。 2000年頃には、 日本とはまったく異なる統計
システムが出来上がっていた。
私たちは、 自分たちが世界の ト ップを走っていると思
い込んでいた。 しかし、 実際には 「周回遅れ」 だったので
ある。
帰国後、 私はアメリカで見聞きしたことと、 日本の統計
制度の問題点を行政関係者の前で報告した。 正直なと
ころ、 「問題を指摘したところで、 どうせ何も変わらない
だろう」 と半ば諦めていた。
ところが、 その予想は良い意味で裏切られた。 日本の
統計行政を担う官僚たちは、 問題の本質を理解すると、
驚くほどの速度と粘り強さで改革を進めた。 既存の調査

を部分的に手直しするのではなく、 産業統計を作成する
システムそのものの再構築に取り組んだのである。
商業 ・ 法人登記等の行政記録の活用が始まり、 2007
年に成立した新しい統計法の下では、 日本版ビジネスレ
ジスターというべき 「事業所母集団データベース」 の整
備が進められた。 日本標準産業分類も大幅に改定され、
企業組織の構造を捉える仕組みも整備された。 こう した
改革を基盤と して、 「経済センサス ‐ 活動調査」 が2012年
に実現した。 現在の日本の産業統計の調査システムは、
国際的に見ても高い水準にある。 一方、 行政記録への依
存を強めた国々では、 統計調査そのものの能力が低下
し、 回収率の低下などによって実態把握が難しくなると
いう新たな問題も生じている。
振り返れば、 日本の統計制度改革は、 自分たちがすで
に大きく遅れているという事実を直視するところから始
まった。 重要なのは、 遅れていたことではない。 それを認
めた後、 従来の仕組みに固執せず、 時間をかけて制度そ

のものを作り替えたことである。
近年、 日本の衰退を憂える声をよく耳にする。 しかし、
挽回はいく らでも可能だと私は思う。 まず必要なのは、
自分たちが置かれた現実を直視することである。 遅れて
いること自体が致命的なのではない。 「まだ自分たちは
一流だ」 と思い込み、 現実から目をそらすことの方がは
るかに危険である。
そして、 現実を変える仕事は若い世代に思う存分やっ
てもらえばよい。 そのための権限と機会を与えるのが上
の世代の仕事である。 任せると決めたなら、 本当に任せ
る。 経験は伝えても、 いつまでも現場の中心に居座らな
い。 日本の統計制度が 「周回遅れ」 から追いつくことがで
きたように、 現実を直視し、 次の世代に改革を委ねること
ができれば、 挽回する力はまだ十分にある。
1 第一ライフ研レポー ト 2026.11
時評
時評

## Document 736: 海外宏观周报：美联储如期暂缓加息

Source PDF: all report pdf/海外宏观周报：美联储如期暂缓加息.pdf

Date: 2026-11-03

### Page 1

证券研究报告：宏观报告

市场有风险，投资需谨慎                                                        请务必阅读正文之后的免责条款部分 
研究所

分析师:李起 
SAC登记编号:S1340524110001 
Email:liqi2@cnpsec.com 
分析师:高晓洁 
SAC登记编号:S1340526020001 
Email:gaoxiaojie@cnpsec.com  
近期研究报告

《海外宏观周报：美联储或按兵不动》 
- 2026.10.27   
 宏观观点

海外宏观周报：美联储如期暂缓加息

⚫ 核心观点 
上周美联储如期暂停加息，声明及新闻发布会的措辞较此前温
和。本次会议仅有库克和哈玛克投下反对票，支持加息 25个基点，
对比此前点阵图所反映的政策倾向来看，本次会议释放的政策信号相
对偏鸽。这可能与核心PCE数据变化以及两次会议之间债券收益率明
显上升有关。沃什在新闻发布会上表示，经济并未过热，政策目标也
不是刻意压低经济活动，显示美联储愿意留出时间，观察此前加息及
融资成本上升对经济和通胀的影响。 
我们倾向于认为，年底前仍可能再加息一次，中东局势及能源价
格向其他商品和服务的传导是主要风险。展望明年，若能源冲击逐渐
减弱，高融资成本对消费和投资的抑制作用进一步显现，美联储仍有
可能转向降息。债券方面，偏温和的政策沟通有助于缓解短端利率的
上行压力，但近期期限溢价已明显收窄，长端收益率进一步下行的空
间有限，建议关注中短期限美债的配置价值。 
美股方面，配置重点应落在能够兑现现金流的企业上。科技企业
财报显示，AI需求仍有支撑，但资本开支持续扩张，正加大企业的资
金压力。建议优先关注订单可见度高、能保持正向自由现金流、对外
部融资依赖较低的企业。

⚫ 风险提示： 
若中东冲突升级，能源涨价向核心通胀的传导超出预期，美联储
鹰派立场可能持续更久，明年可能进一步加息。

发布时间：2026-11-03

1 海外宏观事件与数据跟踪 
上周中东外交斡旋有所推进，但军事准备和海上袭击仍在持续，局势仍然紧
张。周初，沙特、巴基斯坦和土耳其代表赴德黑兰与伊朗官员会谈，随后，卡塔
尔推动以恢复对伊朗核设施的核查，换取美国给予石油出口豁免、释放部分冻结
资金的方案。美国副总统万斯也通过卡塔尔向伊朗递交信函，提议直接会谈。周
末，伊朗代表团赴多哈继续磋商，但双方在核问题、解除港口封锁及释放资金等
条件上仍有分歧，尚未达成协议。军事方面，据媒体报道，美国已向部分海湾盟
友通报可能开展新一轮打击的准备，目标可能涉及伊朗能源、军事及核设施。国
务卿鲁比奥则表示，特朗普尚未作出最终决定。海上运输风险仍然存在：伊朗革
命卫队称有油轮在霍尔木兹海峡触雷起火，卡塔尔一艘液化天然气运输船也于周
五遇袭。另外，中期选举后军事行动可能恢复的担忧也对油价构成支撑。

美国经济增长快于预期，消费和 AI相关投资继续提供支撑。上周公布的第
三季度实际 GDP初值环比折年增长 3.4%，高于预期的 3.2%，也高于第二季度的
2.2%。个人消费支出增长3.8%，非住宅固定投资增长12.7%，其中信息处理设备
投资增长尤为突出。相比之下，住宅投资下降3.3%，显示高按揭利率对住房需求
的压制仍在持续。经济扩张主要依靠消费和企业设备投资，房地产表现偏弱，利
率敏感部门与AI投资相关领域之间的分化延续。

美股科技板块周初反弹，周五再度承压，小盘股相对占优。上周标普500指
数下跌 0.6%，纳斯达克 100指数下跌 2.0%，罗素 2000指数上涨 1.3%。周初半
导体板块曾出现长线资金买入，但随后财报和业绩指引表现不一，周五科技股卖
压明显增加。AI产业链内部，软件及AI智能体相关股票表现偏弱，生物科技和
国防板块相对强势。
